assaddhai puggalai parivaj‍jayatauti buddhādīsu pasādasinaihābhāvaina saddhārahitai lūkhapuggalai sabbasau vaj‍jayatau| saddhai puggalai saivatauti  buddhādīsu saddhādhimuttai vak‍kalitthairasadisai saivatau| pasādanīyaiti pasādāvahai sampasādanīyasuttādikai (dī॰ ni॰ 3.141 ādayau)| pac‍cavaikkhatauti pāḷitau atthatau ca pati pati avaikkhantassa cintaintassa| visujjhatīti paṭipakkhamalavigamatau pac‍cayavasaina sabhāvasaṃsuddhitau visuddhaphalanibbattitau ca saddhindriyaṃ visujjhati| aisa nayau saisaisupi| sammappadhānaiti sammappadhānappaṭisaṃyuttai (saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.651-662 ādayau) suttantai| aisa nayau saisaisupi| jhānavimaukkhaiti paṭhamajjhānādijjhānāni  caiva paṭhamavimaukkhādivimaukkhai ca| kāmañ‍caittha jhānāniyaiva vimaukkhā, pavattiākāravasaina pana visuṃ gahaṇaṃ|

gambhīrañāṇacariyanti gambhīrānaṃ ñāṇānaṃ pavattiṭṭhānaṃ| taināha – ‘‘saṇhasukhuma’’ntiādi| khandhantaranti sabhāvajātibhūmiādivasaina khandhānaṃ nānattaṃ| aisa nayau saisaisupi| akatābhinivaisauti pubbai akatabhāvanābhinivaisau| saddhādhurādīsūti saddhādhurai pañ‍ñādhurai ca| avasānaiti bhāvanāpariyausānai| vivaṭṭaitvāti saṅkhārārammaṇatau vivaṭṭaitvā nibbānaṃ ārammaṇaṃ katvā| arahattaṃ gaṇhātīti maggaparamparāya arahattaṃ gaṇhāti| akampiyaṭṭhaināti paṭipakkhaihi akampiyabhāvaina| aitainaivassa sampayuttadhammaisu thirabhāvaupi vibhāvitau daṭṭhabbau| na hi sampayuttadhammaisu thirabhāvaina vinā paṭipakkhaihi akampiyatā sambhavati| sampayuttadhammaisu thirabhāvainaiva hi akusalānaṃ abyākatānañ‍ca naisaṃ balavabhāvūpapatti| assaddhiyaiti assaddhiyahaitu| nimittatthai haitaṃ bhummavacanaṃ| aisa nayau saisaisupi|

418. ādipadānanti satiādipadānaṃ| saraṇaṭṭhaināti cirakatacirabhāsitānaṃ anussaraṇaṭṭhaina| upaṭṭhānalakkhaṇāti kāyādīsu asubhākārādisal‍lakkhaṇamukhaina tattha upatiṭṭhanasabhāvā| upatiṭṭhanañ‍ca ārammaṇaṃ upagantvā ṭhānaṃ, avissaj‍janaṃ vā ārammaṇassa| apilāpanalakkhaṇāti asammussanasabhāvā, udakai alābu viya ārammaṇai p‍lavitvā gantuṃ appadānaṃ, pāsāṇassa viya nic‍calassa ārammaṇassa ṭhapanaṃ sāraṇaṃ asammuṭṭhakaraṇaṃ apilāpanaṃ| sāpataiyyanti santakaṃ| apilāpanaṃ asammuṭṭhaṃ karauti apilāpaiti, sāyaṃ pātañ‍ca rājānaṃ issariyasampattiṃ sal‍lakkhāpaiti sāraitīti atthau| kaṇhasuk‍kasappaṭibhāgai dhammaiti kaṇhasuk‍kasaṅkhātai sappaṭibhāgai dhammai| kaṇhau hi dhammau suk‍kaina, suk‍kau  ca kaṇhaina sappaṭibhāgau| vitthāra-saddau ādisaddatthau| taina ‘‘imai cattārau dhammā sammappadhānā, imai cattārau iddhipādā, imāni pañ‍cindriyāni, imāni pañ‍ca balāni, imai satta baujjhaṅgā, ayaṃ ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, ayaṃ samathau, ayaṃ vipassanā, ayaṃ vij‍jā, ayaṃ vimutti, imai laukuttaradhammāti aivaṃ khau, mahārāja, apilāpanalakkhaṇā satī’’ti (mi॰ pa॰ 2.1.13) imaṃ pāḷisaisaṃ saṅgaṇhāti| thairaināti nāgasainatthairaina| sau hi dhammānaṃ kic‍caṃ lakkhaṇaṃ katvā assaiti ‘‘apilāpanalakkhaṇā sati, ākauṭanalakkhaṇau vitak‍kau’’tiādinā| aivañhi dhammā subaudhā hauntīti| sammausapac‍canīkaṃ kic‍caṃ asammausau, na sammausābhāvamattanti āha – ‘‘asammausarasā vā’’ti| yassa dhammassa balaina sampayuttadhammā ārammaṇābhimukhā bhavanti, sā sati| tasmā sā taisaṃ ārammaṇābhimukhabhāvaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhāpaisi, sayaṃ vā ārammaṇābhimukhabhāvaina pac‍cupatiṭṭhatīti  vuttaṃ – ‘‘gaucarābhimukhībhāvapac‍cupaṭṭhānā’’ti| sammā pasatthau baujjhaṅgauti sambaujjhaṅgau| baudhiyā vakkhamānāya dhammasāmaggiyā, baudhissa vā ariyasāvakassa aṅgauti baujjhaṅgau| yā hītiādinā tamaiva saṅkhaipatau vuttamatthaṃ vivarati| ‘‘yā hi ayaṃ dhammasāmaggī’’ti aitassa ‘‘baudhīti vuc‍catī’’ti iminā sambandhau| dhammasāmaggiyāti dhammasamūhaina, yāya dhammasāmaggiyāti sambandhau| patiṭṭhānāyūhanā aughataraṇasuttavaṇṇanāyaṃ (saṃ॰ ni॰ aṭṭha॰ 1.1.1) ‘‘kilaisavasaina patiṭṭhānaṃ, abhisaṅkhāravasaina āyūhanā| taṇhā diṭṭhivasaina patiṭṭhānaṃ, avasaisakilaisābhisaṅkhāraihi āyūhanā| taṇhāvasaina patiṭṭhānaṃ, diṭṭhivasaina āyūhanā| sassatadiṭṭhiyā patiṭṭhānaṃ, ucchaidadiṭṭhiyā āyūhanā| līnavasaina patiṭṭhānaṃ, uddhac‍cavasaina āyūhanā| kāmasukhal‍likānuyaugavasaina patiṭṭhānaṃ, attakilamathānuyaugavasaina āyūhanā| sabbākusalābhisaṅkhāravasaina patiṭṭhānaṃ, sabbalaukiyakusalābhisaṅkhāravasaina āyūhanā’’ti aivaṃ vuttaisu sattasu pakāraisu idha avuttānaṃ vasaina vaiditabbā| paṭipakkhabhūtāyāti aittha līnappatiṭṭhānakāmasukhal‍likānuyaugaucchaidābhinivaisānaṃ dhammavicayavīriyapītippadhānā dhammasāmaggī paṭipakkhau, uddhac‍cāyūhanaattakilamathānuyaugasassatābhinivaisānaṃ passaddhisamādhiupaikkhāpadhānā dhammasāmaggī paṭipakkhau| sati pana ubhayatthāpi icchitabbā| tathā hi sā ‘‘sabbatthikā’’ti vuttā|

kilaisasantānaniddāya  uṭṭhahatīti aitaina sikhāppattavipassanāya sahagatānampi satiādīnaṃ baujjhaṅgabhāvaṃ dassaiti| vuṭṭhānagāminivipassanā hi kilaisai niraudhaintī aiva pavattatīti| cattāri vātiādinā pana maggaphalasahagatānaṃ baujjhaṅgabhāvaṃ dassaiti| sattahi baujjhaṅgaihi bhāvitaihi sac‍cappaṭivaidhau hautīti kathamidaṃ jānitabbanti caudanaṃ sandhāyāha – ‘‘yathāhā’’tiādi| jhānaṅgamaggaṅgādayau viyāti aitaina baudhibaujjhaṅgasaddānaṃ samudāyāvayavavisayataṃ dassaiti| sainaṅgarathaṅgādayau viyāti aitaina puggalapañ‍ñattiyā avij‍jamānapañ‍ñattibhāvaṃ dassaiti|

baudhāya saṃvattantīti baujjhaṅgāti kāraṇatthau aṅgasaddauti katvā vuttaṃ| bujjhantīti baudhiyau, baudhiyau aiva aṅgāni baujjhaṅgānīti vuttaṃ – ‘‘bujjhantīti baujjhaṅgā’’ti| vipassanādīnaṃ kāraṇādīnaṃ bujjhitabbānañ‍ca sac‍cānaṃ anurūpaṃ pac‍cakkhabhāvaina paṭimukhaṃ aviparītatāya sammā ca bujjhantīti aivaṃ atthavisaisadīpakaihi upasaggaihi ‘‘anubujjhantī’’tiādi vuttaṃ| baudhisaddau hi sabbavisaisayuttabujjhanaṃ sāmañ‍ñaina saṅgaṇhāti| saṃ-saddau pasaṃsāyaṃ sundarabhāvai ca dissatīti āha – ‘‘pasatthau sundarau ca baujjhaṅgau sambaujjhaṅgau’’ti|

dhammai  vicinatīti dhammavicayau| tattha dhammaiti catusac‍cadhammai tabbinimuttassa sabhāvadhammassa abhāvatau| tatau aiva sau pavicayalakkhaṇau| aubhāsanarasauti visayaubhāsanarasau| asammuyhanākāraina pac‍cupatiṭṭhatīti asammauhapac‍cupaṭṭhānau|

vīrassa bhāvau, kammaṃ vāti vīriyaṃ| īrayitabbatauti pavattaitabbatau| paggahalakkhaṇanti kausaj‍japakkhai patituṃ adatvā sampayuttadhammānaṃ paggahalakkhaṇaṃ| tatau aiva sampayuttadhammai upatthambhanarasaṃ| anausīdanaṃ asaṃsīdanaṃ|

pīṇayatīti tappaiti| pīṇanakic‍caina sampayuttadhammānaṃ viya taṃsamuṭṭhānapaṇītarūpaihi kāyassābyapanaṃ| pharaṇapītivasaina haitaṃ lakkhaṇaṃ vuttaṃ, tathā rasauti| udaggabhāvau audagyaṃ, taṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhapaitīti audagyapac‍cupaṭṭhānā| ubbaigapītivasaina caitaṃ vuttaṃ|

kāyacittadarathappassambhanatauti kāyadarathassa cittadarathassa ca passambhanatau vūpasamanatau| taināha – ‘‘upasamalakkhaṇā’’ti, kāyacittadarathānaṃ  vūpasamanalakkhaṇāti atthau| kāyauti caittha vaidanādayau tayau khandhā| darathau sārambhau, dukkhadaumanassapac‍cayānaṃ uddhac‍cādikānaṃ kilaisānaṃ, tathāpavattānaṃ vā catun‍naṃ khandhānamaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| darathanimmaddanaina pariḷāhaparipphandanavirahitau sītibhāvau aparipphandanasītibhāvau|

sammā cittassa ṭhapanaṃ samādhānaṃ| avikkhaipau sampayuttānaṃ avikkhittatā| yaina sampayuttā avikkhittā haunti, sau dhammau avikkhaipau| avisārau attanau aiva avisaraṇabhāvau| atha vā vikkhaipappaṭipakkhatāya avikkhaipalakkhaṇau| nhānīyacuṇṇassa udakaṃ viya sampayuttadhammānaṃ sampiṇḍanakic‍catāya avisārabhāvaina lakkhitabbau avisāralakkhaṇau| nivātai dīpac‍ciṭṭhiti viya caitasau ṭhitibhāvaina pac‍cupatiṭṭhatīti cittaṭṭhitipac‍cupaṭṭhānau|

ajjhupaikkhanatauti samappavattaisu assaisu sārathi viya sampayuttadhammānaṃ ajjhupaikkhanatau| paṭisaṅkhānalakkhaṇāti majjhattabhāvai ṭhatvā vīmaṃsanasaṅkhātappaṭisaṅkhānalakkhaṇā| samavāhitalakkhaṇāti samaṃ avisamaṃ yathāsakakic‍caisu sampayuttadhammānaṃ pavattanalakkhaṇā| udāsīnabhāvaina pavattamānāpi saisasampayuttadhammai yathāsakakic‍caisu pavattaiti, yathā rājā tuṇhī nisin‍naupi atthakaraṇai dhammaṭṭhai yathāsakaṃ kic‍caisu appamattau pavattaiti| alīnānuddhatappavattipac‍cayattā ūnādhikanivāraṇarasā| pakkhapātupacchaidanarasāti ‘‘idaṃ nihīnakic‍caṃ  hautu, idaṃ atiraikatarakic‍ca’’nti aivaṃ pakkhapātanavasaina viya pavatti pakkhapātau, taṃ upacchindantī viya hautīti pakkhapātupacchaidanarasā| sampayuttadhammānaṃ sakasakakic‍cai majjhattabhāvaina pac‍cupatiṭṭhatīti majjhattabhāvapac‍cupaṭṭhānau| baujjhaṅgānaṃ uparūpari uppādanamaiva brūhanaṃ vaḍḍhanañ‍cāti āha – ‘‘uppādaitī’’ti|

sati ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca satisampajañ‍ñaṃ, satipadhānaṃ vā sampajañ‍ñaṃ satisampajañ‍ñaṃ| taṃ sabbattha sataukāribhāvāvahattā satisambaujjhaṅgassa uppādāya hauti| yathā pac‍canīkadhammappahānaṃ anurūpadhammasaivanā ca anuppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādāya hauti, aivaṃ satirahitapuggalavivaj‍janā, sataukāripuggalasaivanā, tattha ca yuttatā satisambaujjhaṅgassa uppādāya  hautīti imamatthaṃ dassaiti, ‘‘satisampajañ‍ña’’ntiādinā| sattasu ṭhānaisūti ‘‘abhik‍kantai paṭik‍kantai sampajānakārī hauti, ālaukitai vilaukitai sampajānakārī hauti, samiñ‍jitai pasāritai sampajānakārī hauti, saṅghāṭipattacīvaradhāraṇai sampajānakārī hauti, asitai pītai khāyitai sāyitai sampajānakārī hauti, uc‍cārapassāvakammai sampajānakārī hauti, gatai ṭhitai nisin‍nai suttai jāgaritai bhāsitai tuṇhībhāvai sampajānakārī hautī’’ti (dī॰ ni॰ 2.376; ma॰ ni॰ 1.109) aivaṃ vuttaisu abhik‍kantādīsu sattasu ṭhānaisu| tissadattatthairau nāma yau baudhimaṇḍai suvaṇṇasalākaṃ gahaitvā ‘‘aṭṭhārasasu bhāsāsu katarabhāsāya dhammaṃ kathaimī’’ti parisaṃ padhāraisi| abhayatthairauti dattābhayatthairamāha| abhinivaisanti vipassanābhinivaisaṃ|

paripucchakatāti pariyaugāhaitvā pucchakabhāvau| ācariyai payirupāsitvā pañ‍capi nikāyai saha aṭṭhakathāya pariyaugāhaitvā yaṃ yaṃ tattha gaṇṭhiṭṭhānabhūtaṃ, taṃ taṃ ‘‘idaṃ, bhantai, kathaṃ, imassa kau atthau’’ti khandhāyatanādiatthaṃ pucchantassa hi dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau uppaj‍jati| taināha – ‘‘khandhadhātu…pai॰… bahulatā’’ti|

vatthuvisadakiriyāti aittha cittacaitasikānaṃ pavattiṭṭhānabhāvatau sarīraṃ tappaṭibaddhāni cīvarādīni ca vatthūnīti adhippaitāni| tāni yathā cittassa sukhāvahāni haunti, tathā karaṇaṃ taisaṃ visadakiriyā| taināha – ‘‘ajjhattikabāhirāna’’ntiādi| ussan‍nadausanti vātapittādivasaina upacitadausaṃ| saidamalamakkhitanti saidaina caiva jal‍likāsaṅkhātaina sarīramalaina ca makkhitaṃ| ca-saddaina añ‍ñampi sarīrassa pīḷāvahaṃ ac‍cāsanādiṃ saṅgaṇhāti| saināsanaṃ vāti vā-saddaina malaggahitapattādīnaṃ saṅgahau daṭṭhabbau| paribhaṇḍakaraṇādīhīti ādi-saddaina pattapacanādīnaṃ saṅgahau daṭṭhabbau| avisadaiti vatthumhi avisadai sati, visayabhūtai vā| kathaṃ bhāvanamanuyuttassa  tāni ajjhattikabāhiravatthūni visayau? antarantarā pavattanakacittuppādavasainaiva vuttaṃ| tai hi cittuppādā cittaikaggatāya aparisuddhabhāvāya saṃvattanti| cittacaitasikaisūti nissayādipac‍cayabhūtaisu cittacaitasikaisu| ñāṇampīti api-saddau sampiṇḍanatthau| taina ‘‘na kaivalaṃ vatthuyaiva, atha khau tasmiṃ aparisuddhai ñāṇampi aparisuddhaṃ  hautī’’ti nissayāparisuddhiyā taṃnissitāparisuddhi viya visayassa aparisuddhatāya visayinau aparisuddhiṃ dassaiti|

samabhāvakaraṇanti kic‍catau anūnādhikabhāvakaraṇaṃ| saddhindriyaṃ balavaṃ hauti, saddhaiyyavatthusmiṃ pac‍cayavasaina adhimaukkhakic‍cassa paṭutarabhāvaina pañ‍ñāya avisadatāya vīriyādīnañ‍ca sithilatādinā saddhindriyaṃ balavaṃ hauti| taināha – ‘‘itarāni mandānī’’ti| tatauti tasmā, saddhindriyassa balavabhāvatau itaraisañ‍ca mandattāti atthau| kausaj‍japakkhai patituṃ adatvā sampayuttadhammānaṃ paggaṇhanaṃ anubalappadānaṃ paggahau| paggahauva kic‍caṃ paggahakic‍caṃ| kātuṃ na sak‍kautīti ānaitvā sambandhitabbaṃ| ārammaṇaṃ upagantvā ṭhānaṃ, anissaj‍janaṃ vā upaṭṭhānaṃ| vikkhaipappaṭipakkhau, yaina vā sampayuttā avikkhittā haunti, sau avikkhaipau| rūpagataṃ viya cakkhunā yaina yāthāvatau visayasabhāvaṃ passati, taṃ dassanakic‍caṃ kātuṃ na sak‍kauti balavatā saddhindriyaina adhibhūtattā| sahajātadhammaisu hi indaṭṭhaṃ kāraintānaṃ sahapavattamānānaṃ dhammānaṃ aikadaisatāvasainaiva atthasiddhi, na añ‍ñathā| tasmāti vuttamaivatthaṃ kāraṇabhāvaina pac‍cāmasati| tanti saddhindriyaṃ| dhammasabhāvapac‍cavaikkhaṇaināti yassa saddhaiyyassa vatthunau uḷāratādiguṇai adhimuc‍canassa sātisayappavattiyā saddhindriyaṃ balavaṃ jātaṃ, tassa pac‍cayapac‍cayuppan‍natādivibhāgatau yāthāvatau vīmaṃsanaina| aivañhi aivaṃdhammatānayaina yāthāvasarasatau pariggayhamānai savipphārau adhimaukkhau na hauti ‘‘ayaṃ imaisaṃ dhammānaṃ sabhāvau’’ti parijānanavasaina pañ‍ñābyāpārassa sātisayattā| dhuriyadhammaisu hi yathā saddhāya balavabhāvai pañ‍ñāya mandabhāvau hauti, aivaṃ pañ‍ñāya balavabhāvai saddhāya mandabhāvau hauti| taina vuttaṃ – ‘‘taṃ dhammasabhāvapac‍cavaikkhaṇaina hāpaitabba’’nti|

tathā amanasikāraināti yainākāraina bhāvanamanuyuñ‍jantassa saddhindriyaṃ balavaṃ jātaṃ, tainākāraina bhāvanāya ananuyuñ‍janatauti vuttaṃ hauti| idha duvidhaina saddhindriyassa balavabhāvau attanau vā pac‍cayavisaisaina kic‍cuttariyatau vīriyādīnaṃ vā mandakic‍catāya| tattha paṭhamavikappai hāpanavidhi dassitau, dutiyavikappai pana yathā manasikarautau vīriyādīnaṃ mandakic‍catāya saddhindriyaṃ balavaṃ jātaṃ, tathā amanasikāraina vīriyādīnaṃ  paṭukic‍cabhāvāvahaina manasikāraina  saddhindriyaṃ taihi samarasaṃ karauntaina hāpaitabbaṃ| iminā nayaina saisindriyaisupi hāpanavidhi vaiditabbau|

vak‍kalitthairavatthūti sau hi āyasmā saddhādhimuttatāya katādhikārau satthu rūpadassanappasutau aiva hutvā viharantau satthārā ‘‘kiṃ tai, vak‍kali, iminā pūtikāyaina diṭṭhaina, yau khau, vak‍kali, dhammaṃ passati, sau maṃ passatī’’tiādinā (saṃ॰ ni॰ 3.87) nayaina auvaditvā kammaṭṭhānai niyaujitaupi taṃ ananuyuñ‍jantau paṇāmitau attānaṃ vinipātaituṃ papātaṭṭhānaṃ abhiruhi| atha naṃ satthā yathānisin‍nauva aubhāsavissaj‍janaina attānaṃ dassaitvā –

‘‘pāmauj‍jabahulau bhikkhu, pasan‍nau buddhasāsanai|

adhigacchai padaṃ santaṃ, saṅkhārūpasamaṃ sukha’’nti|| (dha॰ pa॰ 381) –

gāthaṃ vatvā ‘‘aihi, vak‍kalī’’ti āha| sau tainaiva amataina abhisittau haṭṭhatuṭṭhau hutvā vipassanaṃ paṭṭhapaisi, saddhāya pana balavabhāvaina vipassanāvīthiṃ na autari| taṃ ñatvā bhagavā tassa indriyasamattappaṭipādanāya kammaṭṭhānaṃ saudhaitvā adāsi| sau satthārā din‍nanayai ṭhatvā vipassanaṃ ussuk‍kāpaitvā maggappaṭipāṭiyā arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| taina vuttaṃ – ‘‘vak‍kalitthairavatthu caittha nidassana’’nti| aitthāti saddhindriyassa adhimattabhāvai saisindriyānaṃ sakic‍cākaraṇai| itarakic‍cabhaidanti upaṭṭhānādikic‍cavisaisaṃ| passaddhādīti ādi-saddaina samādhiupaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgānaṃ saṅgahau| hāpaitabbanti yathā saddhindriyassa balavabhāvau dhammasabhāvapac‍cavaikkhaṇaina hāyati, aivaṃ vīriyindriyassa adhimattatā passaddhiādibhāvanāya hāyati samādhipakkhiyattā tassā| tathā hi samādhindriyassa adhimattataṃ kausaj‍japātatau rakkhantī vīriyādibhāvanā viya vīriyindriyassa adhimattataṃ uddhac‍capātatau rakkhantī passaddhādibhāvanā aikaṃsatau hāpaiti| taina vuttaṃ – ‘‘passaddhādibhāvanāya hāpaitabba’’nti|

sauṇatthairassa vatthūti sukhumālasauṇatthairassa vatthu| sau hi āyasmā satthu santikai kammaṭṭhānaṃ gahaitvā sītavanai viharantau ‘‘mama sarīraṃ sukhumālaṃ, na ca sak‍kā sukhainaiva sukhaṃ adhigantuṃ, kilamaitvāpi samaṇadhammau  kātabbau’’ti ṭhānacaṅkamamaiva adhiṭṭhāya padhānamanuyuñ‍jantau pādatalaisu phauṭaisu uṭṭhitaisupi vaidanaṃ ajjhupaikkhitvā daḷhavīriyaṃ karauntau ac‍cāraddhavīriyatāya visaisaṃ nibbattaituṃ nāsakkhi| satthā tattha gantvā vīṇūpamauvādaina auvaditvā  vīriyasamatāyaujanavidhiṃ dassaintau kammaṭṭhānaṃ visaudhaitvā gijjhakūṭaṃ gatau| thairaupi satthārā din‍nanayaina vīriyasamataṃ yaujaitvā bhāvaintau vipassanampi ussuk‍kāpaitvā arahattai patiṭṭhāsi| taina vuttaṃ – ‘‘sauṇatthairassa vatthu dassaitabba’’nti| saisaisupīti satisamādhipañ‍ñindriyaisupi|

samatanti saddhāpañ‍ñānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ anūnādhikabhāvaṃ, tathā samādhivīriyānaṃ| yathā hi saddhāpañ‍ñānaṃ visuṃ dhuriyadhammabhūtānaṃ kic‍catau añ‍ñamañ‍ñānativattanaṃ visaisatau icchitabbaṃ, yatau naisaṃ samadhuratāya appanā sampaj‍jati, aivaṃ samādhivīriyānaṃ kausaj‍juddhac‍capakkhikānaṃ samarasatāya sati añ‍ñamañ‍ñūpatthambhanatau sampayuttadhammānaṃ antadvayapātābhāvaina sammadaiva appanā ijjhati| balavasaddhautiādi byatiraikamukhaina vuttassaivatthassa samatthanaṃ| tassatthau – yau balavatiyā saddhāya saman‍nāgatau avisadañāṇau, sau mudhappasan‍nau hauti, na avaic‍cappasan‍nau| tathā hi avatthusmiṃ pasīdati saiyyathāpi titthiyasāvakā| kairāṭikapakkhanti sāṭhaiyyapakkhaṃ bhajati| saddhāhīnāya pañ‍ñāya atidhāvantau ‘‘daiyyavatthuparic‍cāgaina vinā cittuppādamattainapi dānamayaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ hautī’’tiādīni parikappaiti haituppaṭirūpakaihi vañ‍citau, aivaṃbhūtau sukkhatak‍kaviluttacittau paṇḍitānaṃ vacanaṃ nādiyati, sañ‍ñattiṃ na gacchati| taināha – ‘‘bhaisaj‍jasamuṭṭhitau viya raugau ataikicchau hautī’’ti| yathā caittha saddhāpañ‍ñānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ samabhāvau atthāvahau, anatthāvahau visamabhāvau, aivaṃ samādhivīriyānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ avikkhaipāvahau samabhāvau, itarau vikkhaipāvahau cāti kausaj‍jaṃ abhibhavati, taina appanaṃ na pāpuṇātīti adhippāyau| uddhac‍caṃ abhibhavatīti aitthāpi aisaiva nayau| taṃ ubhayanti saddhāpañ‍ñādvayaṃ samādhivīriyadvayañ‍ca| samaṃ kattabbanti samarasaṃ kātabbaṃ|

samādhikammikassāti samathakammaṭṭhānikassa| aivanti aivaṃ santai, saddhāya thaukaṃ balavabhāvai satīti atthau| saddahantauti ‘‘pathavī pathavīti manasikaraṇamattaina  kathaṃ jhānuppattī’’ti acintaitvā ‘‘addhā sammāsambuddhaina vuttavidhi ijjhissatī’’ti saddahantau saddhaṃ janaintau| aukappaintauti ārammaṇaṃ anuppavisitvā viya adhimuc‍canavasaina avakappaintau pakkhandantau| aikaggatā balavatī vaṭṭati samādhippadhānattā jhānassa| ubhin‍nanti samādhipañ‍ñānaṃ| samādhikammikassa samādhinau adhimattatā viya pañ‍ñāya adhimattatāpi icchitabbāti āha – ‘‘samatāyapī’’ti, samabhāvainapīti atthau| appanāti laukiyaappanā| tathā hi ‘‘hautiyaivā’’ti sāsaṅkaṃ vadati| laukuttarappanā pana taisaṃ samabhāvainaiva icchitā| yathāha – ‘‘samathavipassanaṃ yuganaddhaṃ bhāvaitī’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 4.170; paṭi॰ ma॰ 2.5)|

yadi  visaisatau saddhāpañ‍ñānaṃ samādhivīriyānañ‍ca samatāva icchitā, kathaṃ satīti āha – ‘‘sati pana sabbattha balavatī vaṭṭatī’’ti| sabbatthāti līnuddhac‍capakkhikaisu pañ‍casu indriyaisu| uddhac‍capakkhikai gaṇhantau ‘‘saddhāvīriyapañ‍ñāna’’nti āha| añ‍ñathāpīti ca gahaitabbā siyā| tathā hi kausaj‍japakkhikaina ca samādhināic‍caiva vuttaṃ, na ‘‘passaddhisamādhiupaikkhāhī’’ti| sāti sati| sabbaisu rājakammaisu niyuttau sabbakammikau| taināti taina sabbattha icchitabbatthaina kāraṇaina| āha aṭṭhakathāyaṃ| sabbattha niyuttā sabbatthikā sabbattha līnai uddhatai ca cittai icchitabbattā, sabbaina vā līnuddhac‍capakkhiyaina baujjhaṅgagaṇaina atthaitabbāti sabbatthā, sāva sabbatthikā| cittanti kusalacittaṃ| tassa hi satipaṭisaraṇaṃ parāyaṇaṃ appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa adhigamāya| taināha – ‘‘ārakkhapac‍cupaṭṭhānā’’tiādi|

khandhādibhaidaisu anaugāḷhapañ‍ñānanti pariyattibāhusac‍cavasainapi khandhāyatanādīsu appatiṭṭhitabuddhīnaṃ| bahussutasaivanā hi sutamayañāṇāvahā| taruṇavipassanāsamaṅgīpi bhāvanāmayañāṇai ṭhitattā aikaṃsatau pañ‍ñavā aiva nāma hautīti āha – ‘‘samapañ‍ñāsa…pai॰… puggalasaivanā’’ti| ñaiyyadhammassa gambhīrabhāvavasaina tapparicchaidakañāṇassa gambhīrabhāvaggahaṇanti āha – ‘‘gambhīraisu khandhādīsu pavattāya gambhīrapañ‍ñāyā’’ti| tañhi ñaiyyaṃ tādisāya pañ‍ñāya caritabbatau gambhīrañāṇacariyaṃ| tassā vā pañ‍ñāya tattha pabhaidatau pavatti gambhīrañāṇacariyā, tassā pac‍cavaikkhaṇāti āha – ‘‘gambhīrapañ‍ñāya pabhaidapac‍cavaikkhaṇā’’ti|

pañ‍cavidhabandhanakammakāraṇaṃ  nirayai nibbattasattassa yaibhuyyaina sabbapaṭhamaṃ karauntīti daivadūtasuttādīsu āditau vuttattā ca āha – ‘‘pañ‍cavidhabandhanakammakāraṇatau paṭṭhāyā’’ti| sakaṭavahanādikālaiti ādi-saddaina tadañ‍ñaṃ manussaihi tiracchānaihi ca vibādhitabbakālaṃ saṅgaṇhāti| aikaṃ buddhantaranti idaṃ aparāparaisu paitaisuyaiva uppaj‍janakasattavasaina vuttaṃ, aikac‍cānaṃ vā paitānaṃ aikac‍catiracchānānaṃ viya dīghāyukatā siyāti tathā vuttaṃ| tathā hi kāḷau nāgarājā catun‍naṃ buddhānaṃ sammukhībhāvaṃ labhitvā ṭhitaupi maittaiyyassapi bhagavatau sammukhībhāvaṃ labhissatīti vadanti, yatassa kappāyukatā vuttā|

aivaṃ ānisaṃsadassāvinauti vīriyāyattau aiva sabbau laukiyau laukuttarau ca visaisādhigamauti aivaṃ vīriyai ānisaṃsadassanasīlassa| gamanavīthinti sapubbabhāgaṃ nibbānagāminipaṭipadaṃ, saha vipassanāya ariyamaggappaṭipāṭi, sattavisuddhiparamparā vā| sā hi ‘‘bhikkhunau vaṭṭaniyyānāya gantabbā paṭipaj‍jitabbā paṭipadā’’ti katvā gamanavīthi nāma|

kāyadaḷhībahulauti yathā tathā kāyassa daḷhīkammappasutau| piṇḍapātanti raṭṭhapiṇḍaṃ| pac‍cayadāyakānaṃ attani kārassa attanau sammāpaṭipattiyā mahapphalabhāvassa karaṇaina piṇḍassa bhikkhāya paṭipūjanā piṇḍapātāpacāyanaṃ|

nīharantauti pattatthavikatau nīharantau| taṃ saddaṃ sutvāti taṃ upāsikāya vacanaṃ paṇṇasālādvārai ṭhitauva pañ‍cābhiñ‍ñatāya dibbasautaina sutvā| manussasampatti, dibbasampatti, nibbānasampattīti imā tissau sampattiyau| dātuṃ sakkhissasīti tayi kataina dānamayaina vaiyyāvac‍camayaina ca puñ‍ñakammaina khaittavisaisabhāvūpagamanaina aparāparaṃ daivamanussānaṃ sampattiyau antai nibbānasampattiñ‍ca dātuṃ sakkhissasīti thairau attānaṃ pucchati| sitaṃ karauntauti ‘‘akicchainaiva mayā vaṭṭadukkhaṃ samatik‍kanta’’nti pac‍cavaikkhaṇāvasānai sañ‍jātapāmauj‍javasaina sitaṃ karauntau|

nipparissayakālauti nirupaddavakālau, tadā bhikkhusaṅghassa sulabhā pac‍cayā hauntīti pac‍cayahaitukā cittapīḷā natthīti adhippāyau| passantānaṃyaivāti anādarai sāmivacanaṃ| khīradhainunti khīradāyikaṃ dhainuṃ| kiñ‍cidaiva katvāti kiñ‍cidaiva bhatikammaṃ katvā| ucchuyantakammanti ucchuyantasālāya kātabbaṃ kic‍caṃ| tamaiva magganti upāsakaina paṭipan‍namaggaṃ| upakaṭṭhāyāti āsan‍nāya| vippaṭipan‍nanti jātidhammakuladhammādilaṅghanaina asammāpaṭipan‍naṃ| aivanti yathā asammāpaṭipan‍nau puttau tāya aiva asammāpaṭipattiyā kulasantānatau bāhirau hutvā pitu santikā dāyaj‍jassa na bhāgī, aivaṃ kusītaupi tainaiva kusītabhāvaina na sammāpaṭipan‍nau satthu santikā laddhabbaariyadhanadāyaj‍jassa na bhāgī| āraddhavīriyauva labhati sammāpaṭipaj‍janatau| uppaj‍jati vīriyasambaujjhaṅgauti yaujanā, aivaṃ sabbattha|

mahāti sīlādiguṇaihi mahantau vipulau anañ‍ñasādhāraṇau| taṃ panassa guṇamahattaṃ dasasahassilaukadhātukampanaina laukai pākaṭanti dassaintau ‘‘satthunau hī’’tiādimāha|

yasmā  satthusāsanai pabbajitassa pabbaj‍jūpagamanaina sakyaputtiyabhāvau sañ‍jāyati, tasmā buddhaputtabhāvaṃ dassaintau ‘‘asambhin‍nāyā’’tiādimāha|

alasānaṃ bhāvanāya nāmamattampi ajānantānaṃ kāyadaḷhībahulānaṃ yāvadatthaṃ bhuñ‍jitvā saiyyasukhādianuyuñ‍janakānaṃ tiracchānagatikānaṃ puggalānaṃ dūratau vaj‍janā kusītapuggalaparivaj‍janāti āha – ‘‘kucchiṃ pūraitvā ṭhitaajagarasadisai’’tiādi| ‘‘divasaṃ caṅkamaina nisaj‍jāya āvaraṇīyaihi dhammaihi cittaṃ parisaudhaissāmā’’tiādinā bhāvanārambhavasaina āraddhavīriyānaṃ daḷhaparak‍kamānaṃ kālaina kālaṃ upasaṅkamanā āraddhavīriyapuggalasaivanāti āha – ‘‘āraddhavīriyai’’tiādi| visuddhimaggai (visuddhi॰ 1.64-65) pana jātimahattapac‍cavaikkhaṇā sabrahmacārimahattapac‍cavaikkhaṇāti idaṃ dvayaṃ na gahitaṃ, thinamiddhavinaudanatā sammappadhānapac‍cavaikkhaṇatāti idaṃ dvayaṃ gahitaṃ| tattha ānisaṃsadassāvitāya aiva sammappadhānapac‍cavaikkhaṇā gahitā hauti laukiyalaukuttaravisaisādhigamassa vīriyāyattatādassanabhāvatau, thinamiddhavinaudanaṃ pana tadadhimuttatāya aiva gahitaṃ hauti| vīriyuppādanai yuttappayuttassa thinamiddhavinaudanaṃ atthasiddhamaivāti| tattha  thinamiddhavinaudanakusītapuggalaparivaj‍janaāraddhavīriyapuggalasaivanatadadhi- muttatāpaṭipakkhavidhamanapac‍cayūpasaṃhāravasaina apāyabhayapac‍cavaikkhaṇādayau samuttaijanavasaina vīriyasambaujjhaṅgassa uppādakā daṭṭhabbā|

buddhānussatiyā upacārasamādhiniṭṭhattā vuttaṃ – ‘‘yāva upacārā’’ti| sakalasarīraṃ pharamānauti pītisamuṭṭhānaihi paṇītarūpaihi sakalasarīraṃ pharamānau, dhammasaṅghaguṇai anussarantassapi yāva upacārā sakalasarīraṃ pharamānau pītisambaujjhaṅgau uppaj‍jatīti aivaṃ saisaanussatīsu, pasādanīyasuttantapac‍cavaikkhaṇāya ca yaujaitabbaṃ tassāpi vimuttāyatanabhāvaina taggatikattā| aivarūpai kālaiti ‘‘dubbhikkhabhayādīsū’’ti vuttakālai| samāpatti…pai॰… na samudācarantīti idaṃ upasamānussatiyā vasaina vuttaṃ| saṅkhārānañhi vasaina sappadaisavūpasamaipi nippadaisavūpasamai viya tathā sañ‍ñāya pavattitau bhāvanāmanasikārau kilaisavikkhambhanasamatthau hutvā upacārasamādhiṃ āvahantau tathārūpapītisaumanassasaman‍nāgatau pītisambaujjhaṅgassa upādāya hautīti| tattha ‘‘vikkhambhitā kilaisā’’ti pāṭhau| na samudācarantīti iti-saddau kāraṇatthau| yasmā na samudācaranti, tasmā taṃ naisaṃ asamudācāraṃ pac‍cavaikkhantassāti yaujanā| na hi kilaisai pac‍cavaikkhantassa baujjhaṅguppatti yuttā, pasādanīyaisu ṭhānaisu pasādasinaihābhāvaina lūkhahadayatāya lūkhatā| sā tattha ādaragāravākaraṇaina viñ‍ñāyatīti āha – ‘‘asak‍kac‍cakiriyāya saṃsūcitalūkhabhāvai’’ti|

paṇītabhaujanasaivanatāti  paṇītasappāyabhaujanasaivanatā| utuiriyāpathasukhaggahaṇaina sappāyautuiriyāpathaggahaṇaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| tañhi tividhampi sappāyaṃ saiviyamānaṃ kāyassa kalyatāpādanavasaina cittassa kalyataṃ āvahantaṃ duvidhāyapi passaddhiyā kāraṇaṃ hauti| ahaitukasattaisu labbhamānaṃ sukhadukkhanti ayamaikau antau, issarādivisamahaitukanti pana ayaṃ dutiyau| aitai ubhau antai anupagamma yathāsakaṃ kammunā hautīti ayaṃ majjhimā paṭipatti| majjhattau payaugau yassa sau majjhattapayaugau, tassa bhāvau majjhattapayaugatā| ayañhi sabhāvāsāraddhatāya taṃpassaddhakāyatāya kāraṇaṃ hauti, passaddhidvayaṃ  āvahati| aitainaiva sāraddhakāyapuggalaparivaj‍janapassaddhakāyapuggalasaivanānaṃ tadāvahanatā saṃvaṇṇitāti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

vatthuvisadakiriyā indriyasamattappaṭipādanā ca pañ‍ñāvahā vuttā, samādhānāvahāpi tā haunti| samādhānāvahabhāvainaiva pañ‍ñāvahabhāvatauti vuttaṃ – ‘‘vatthuvisadakiriyā…pai॰… vaiditabbā’’ti|

kāraṇakausal‍labhāvanākausal‍lānaṃ nānantariyabhāvatau rakkhanākausal‍lassa ca taṃmūlakattā ‘‘nimittakusalatā nāma kasiṇanimittassa uggahakusalatā’’ic‍caiva vuttaṃ| kasiṇanimittassāti ca nidassanamattaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| asubhanimittādikassapi hi yassa kassaci jhānuppattinimittassa uggahakausal‍laṃ nimittakusalatā aivāti| atisithilavīriyatādīhīti ādi-saddaina pañ‍ñāpayaugamandataṃ payaugavaikal‍lañ‍ca saṅgaṇhāti| tassa paggaṇhananti tassa līnassa cittassa dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgādisamuṭṭhāpanaina layāpattitau samuddharaṇaṃ| vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatā –

‘‘yasmiñ‍ca khau, bhikkhavai, samayai līnaṃ cittaṃ hauti, kālau tasmiṃ samayai dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya, kālau vīriyasambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya, kālau pītisambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya| taṃ kissa haitu? līnaṃ, bhikkhavai, cittaṃ, taṃ aitaihi dhammaihi susamuṭṭhāpayaṃ hauti| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau parittaṃ aggiṃ uj‍jālaitukāmau assa| sau tattha sukkhāni caiva tiṇāni pakkhipaiyya, sukkhāni ca gaumayāni pakkhipaiyya, sukkhāni ca kaṭṭhāni pakkhipaiyya, mukhavātañ‍ca dadaiyya, na ca paṃsukaina aukiraiyya, bhabbau nu khau sau purisau parittaṃ aggiṃ uj‍jālaitunti? aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti (saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.234)|

aittha  ca yathāsakaṃ āhāravasaina dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgādīnaṃ bhāvanā samuṭṭhāpanāti vaiditabbā, sā anantaraṃ vibhāvitā aiva|

ac‍cāraddhavīriyatādīhīti ādi-saddaina pañ‍ñāpayaugabalavataṃ pamauduppilāpanañ‍ca saṅgaṇhāti| tassa niggaṇhananti tassa uddhatassa cittassa samādhisambaujjhaṅgādisamuṭṭhāpanaina uddhatāpattitau nisaidhanaṃ| vuttampi caitaṃ bhagavatā –

‘‘yasmiñ‍ca  khau, bhikkhavai, samayai uddhataṃ cittaṃ hauti, kālau tasmiṃ samayai passaddhisambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya, kālau samādhisambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya, kālau upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya| taṃ kissa haitu? uddhataṃ, bhikkhavai, cittaṃ, taṃ aitaihi dhammaihi suvūpasamaṃ hauti| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau mahantaṃ aggikkhandhaṃ nibbāpaitukāmau assa, sau tattha al‍lāni caiva tiṇāni pakkhipaiyya, al‍lāni ca gaumayāni nikkhipaiyya, al‍lāni ca kaṭṭhāni pakkhipaiyya, mukhavātañ‍ca na dadaiyya, paṃsukaina ca aukiraiyya, bhabbau nu khau sau purisau mahantaṃ aggikkhandhaṃ nibbāpaitunti? aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti (saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.234)|

aitthāpi yathāsakaṃ āhāravasaina passaddhisambaujjhaṅgādīnaṃ bhāvanā samuṭṭhāpanāti vaiditabbā| tattha passaddhisambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanā vuttā aiva, samādhisambaujjhaṅgassa vuc‍camānā, itarassa anantaraṃ vakkhati|

pañ‍ñāpayaugamandatāyāti pañ‍ñābyāpārassa appakabhāvaina| yathā hi dānaṃ alaubhappadhānaṃ, sīlaṃ adausappadhānaṃ, aivaṃ bhāvanā amauhappadhānā| tattha yadā pañ‍ñā na balavatī hauti, tadā bhāvanā pubbaināparaṃ visaisāvahā na hauti| anabhisaṅgatau viya āhārau purisassa, yauginau cittassa abhiruciṃ na janaiti, taina taṃ nirassādaṃ hauti| tathā bhāvanāya sammadaiva avīthipaṭipattiyā upasamasukhaṃ na vindati, tainapi cittaṃ nirassādaṃ hauti| taina vuttaṃ – ‘‘pañ‍ñāpayaugamandatāyā…pai॰… nirassādaṃ hautī’’ti| tassa saṃvaiguppādanaṃ pasāduppādanañ‍ca tikicchananti taṃ dassaintau, ‘‘aṭṭha saṃvaigavatthūnī’’tiādimāha| tattha jātijarābyādhimaraṇāni yathārahaṃ sugatiyaṃ duggatiyañ‍ca hauntīti tadañ‍ñamaiva pañ‍cavidhabandhanādikhuppipāsādiañ‍ñamañ‍ñavihaiṭhanādihaitukaṃ apāyadukkhaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| tayidaṃ sabbaṃ taisaṃ taisaṃ sattānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nabhavanissitaṃ gahitanti atītai anāgatai ca kālai vaṭṭamūlakadukkhāni visuṃ gahitāni| yai pana sattā āhārūpajīvinau, tattha ca uṭṭhānaphalūpajīvinau, taisaṃ añ‍ñaihi asādhāraṇajīvitadukkhaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ saṃvaigavatthu gahitanti daṭṭhabbaṃ| ayaṃ vuc‍cati samayai sampahaṃsanatāti ayaṃ bhāvanācittassa sampahaṃsitabbasamayai vuttanayainaiva saṃvaigajananavasaina  caiva pasāduppādanavasaina ca sammadaiva pahaṃsanā, saṃvaigajananapubbakapasāduppādanaina tausanāti atthau|

sammāpaṭipattiṃ āgammāti līnuddhac‍cavirahaina samathavīthipaṭipattiyā ca sammā avisamaṃ sammadaiva bhāvanāpaṭipattiṃ āgamma| alīnantiādīsu kausaj‍japakkhiyānaṃ dhammānaṃ anadhimattatāya alīnaṃ, uddhac‍capakkhikānaṃ anadhimattatāya anuddhataṃ, pañ‍ñāpayaugasampattiyā upasamasukhādhigamaina ca anirassādaṃ, tatau aiva ārammaṇai samappavattaṃ samathavīthipaṭipan‍naṃ| alīnānuddhatāhi vā ārammaṇai samappavattaṃ, anirassādatāya samathavīthipaṭipan‍naṃ| samappavattiyā vā alīnaṃ anuddhataṃ, samathavīthipaṭipattiyā anirassādanti daṭṭhabbaṃ| tattha alīnatāya paggahai, anuddhatatāya niggahai, anirassādatāya sampahaṃsanai na byāpāraṃ āpaj‍jati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati samayai ajjhupaikkhanatāti ayaṃ ajjhupaikkhitabbasamayai bhāvanācittassa paggahaniggahasampahaṃsanaisu byāvaṭatāsaṅkhātaṃ paṭipakkhaṃ abhibhuyya ajjhupaikkhanā vuc‍cati| paṭipakkhavikkhambhanatau vipassanāya adhiṭṭhānabhāvūpagamanatau ca upacārajjhānampi samādhānakic‍canipphattiyā puggalassa samāhitabhāvasādhanamaivāti tattha samadhurabhāvaināha – ‘‘upacāraṃ vā appanaṃ vā’’ti| aisa uppaj‍jatīti aisa samādhisambaujjhaṅgau anuppan‍nau uppaj‍jati|

anuraudhaviraudhappahānavasaina majjhattabhāvau upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgassa kāraṇaṃ tasmiṃ sati sijjhanatau, asati ca asijjhanatau, sau ca majjhattabhāvau visayavasaina duvidhauti āha – ‘‘sattamajjhattatā saṅkhāramajjhattatā’’ti| tadubhayaina ca virujjhanaṃ passaddhisambaujjhaṅgabhāvanāya aiva dūrīkatanti anurujjhanassaiva pahānavidhiṃ dassaituṃ – ‘‘sattamajjhattatā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| taināha – ‘‘sattasaṅkhārakailāyanapuggalaparivaj‍janatā’’ti| upaikkhāya hi visaisatau rāgau paṭipakkhau| tathā cāha – ‘‘upaikkhā rāgabahulassa visuddhimaggau’’ti (visuddhi॰ 1.269)| dvīhākāraihīti kammassakatāpac‍cavaikkhaṇaṃ, attasuñ‍ñatāpac‍cavaikkhaṇanti, imaihi dvīhi kāraṇaihi| dvīhaivāti avadhāraṇaṃ saṅkhyāsamānatādassanatthaṃ| saṅkhyā aiva haittha samānā, na saṅkhyaiyyaṃ sabbathā samānanti| assāmikabhāvau anattaniyatā| sati hi attani tassa kiñ‍canabhāvaina cīvaraṃ añ‍ñaṃ vā kiñ‍ci attaniyaṃ nāma siyā, sau  pana kauci natthaivāti adhippāyau| anaddhaniyanti na addhānakkhamaṃ, na ciraṭṭhāyī ittaraṃ anic‍canti atthau| tāvakālikanti tassaiva vaivacanaṃ|

mamāyatīti  mamattaṃ karauti, mamāti taṇhāya pariggayha tiṭṭhati| dhanāyantāti dhanaṃ dabbaṃ karauntā|

419. sammādassanalakkhaṇāti sammā aviparītaṃ anic‍cādivasaina dassanasabhāvā| sammāabhiniraupanalakkhaṇauti sammadaiva ārammaṇai cittassa abhiniraupanasabhāvau| caturaṅgasaman‍nāgatā vācā janaṃ saṅgaṇhātīti tabbipakkhaviratisabhāvā sammāvācā bhaidakaramicchāvācāpahānaina janai sampayuttai ca pariggaṇhanakic‍cavatī hautīti ‘‘pariggahalakkhaṇā’’ti vuttā| visaṃvādanādikic‍catāya hi lūkhānaṃ apariggāhakānaṃ musāvādādīnaṃ paṭipakkhabhūtā siniddhabhāvaina pariggahaṇasabhāvā sammājappanakic‍cā sammāvācā tappac‍cayasubhāsitasampaṭiggāhakai janai sampayuttadhammai ca pariggaṇhantī pavattatīti pariggahalakkhaṇā| yathā cīvarakammādippayaugasaṅkhātau kammantau kātabbaṃ cīvararajanādikaṃ samuṭṭhāpaiti nipphādaiti, taṃtaṃkiriyānipphādakau vā caitanāsaṅkhātau kammantau hatthacalanādikaṃ kiriyaṃ samuṭṭhāpaiti, aivaṃ sāvaj‍jakattabbakiriyāsamuṭṭhāpakamicchākammantappahānaina sammākammantau niravaj‍jassa kattabbassa niravaj‍jākāraina samuṭṭhāpanakic‍cavā hautīti āha – ‘‘sammāsamuṭṭhāpanalakkhaṇau’’ti| sampayuttadhammānaṃ vā ukkhipanaṃ samuṭṭhāpanaṃ kāyikakiriyāya bhārukkhipanaṃ viya| sammāvaudāpanalakkhaṇauti jīvamānassa puggalassa, sampayuttadhammānaṃ vā jīvitindriyavuttiyā, ājīvassaiva vā sammadaiva saudhanaṃ vaudāpanaṃ lakkhaṇaṃ aitassāti sammāvaudāpanalakkhaṇau| atha vā kāyavācānaṃ khandhasantānassa ca saṃkilaisabhūtamicchāājīvappahānaina sammāājīvau ‘‘vaudāpanalakkhaṇau’’ti vuttau| sammāvāyāmasatisamādhīsu vattabbaṃ haiṭṭhā vuttamaiva|

pañ‍ñāya kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pubbaṅgamabhāvatau sabbaipi akusalā dhammā tassā paṭipakkhāvāti vuttaṃ – ‘‘añ‍ñaihipi attanau pac‍canīkakilaisaihi saddhi’’nti| atha vā attanau pac‍canīkakilaisā diṭṭhaikaṭṭhā avij‍jādayau pañ‍ñāya ujupac‍canīkabhāvatau| passatīti passantī  viya hauti vibandhābhāvatau| taināha – ‘‘tappaṭicchādaka…pai॰… asammauhatau’’ti| sammāsaṅkappādīnaṃ micchāsaṅkappādayau ujuvipac‍canīkāti āha – ‘‘sammāsaṅkappādayau…pai॰… pajahantī’’ti| tathaivāti iminā attanau pac‍canīkakilaisaihi saddhinti imamatthaṃ anukaḍḍhati| visaisatauti sammādiṭṭhiyā vuttakic‍catau visaisaina| aitthāti aitaisu sammāsaṅkappādīsu|

aisā sammādiṭṭhi nāmāti laukiyaṃ laukuttarañ‍ca aikajjhaṃ katvā vadati missakatābhāvatau| taināha – ‘‘pubbabhāgai’’tiādi| aikārammaṇā nibbānārammaṇattā| kic‍catauti pubbabhāgai dukkhādīhi ñāṇaihi kātabbakic‍cassa idha nipphattitau, imassaiva vā ñāṇassa dukkhādippakāsanakic‍catau| cattāri nāmāni labhati dukkhapariñ‍ñādicatukic‍casādhanatau| tīṇi nāmāni labhati kāmasaṅkappādippahānakic‍canipphattitau| sikkhāpadavibhaṅgai (vibha॰ 703 ādayau) ‘‘viraticaitanā taṃsampayuttā ca dhammā sikkhāpadānī’’ti vuttāni, tattha padhānānaṃ viraticaitanānaṃ vasaina ‘‘viratiyaupi haunti caitanādayaupī’’ti āha| ‘‘sammā vadati aitāyā’’tiādinā atthasambhavatau sammāvācādayau tayau viratiyaupi haunti caitanādayaupi| musāvādādīhi viramaṇakālai viratiyau, subhāsitādivācābhāsanādikālai caitanādayau yaujaitabbā| maggakkhaṇai pana viratiyauva maggalakkhaṇappattitau| na hi caitanā niyyānasabhāvā| atha vā aikassa ñāṇassa dukkhādiñāṇatā viya aikāya viratiyā musāvādādiviratibhāvau viya ca aikāya caitanāya sammāvācādikic‍cattayasādhanasabhāvā sammāvācādibhāvāsiddhitau ‘‘maggakkhaṇai viratiyauvā’’ti vuttaṃ|

cattāri nāmāni labhatīti catusammappadhānacatusatipaṭṭhānavasaina labhati| maggakkhaṇaiti ānaitvā sambandhau| pubbabhāgaipi maggakkhaṇaipi sammāsamādhi aivāti yadipi samādhiupakārakānaṃ abhiniraupanānumaj‍janasampiyāyanabrūhanasantasukhānaṃ vitak‍kādīnaṃ vasaina catūhi jhānaihi sammāsamādhi vibhattau, tathāpi vāyāmau viya anuppan‍nākusalānuppādanādicatuvāyāmakic‍caṃ, sati viya ca asubhāsukhānic‍cānattaisu kāyādīsu subhādisañ‍ñāpahānalakkhaṇaṃ catusatikic‍caṃ, aikau samādhi catujjhānasamādhikic‍caṃ na sādhaitīti pubbabhāgaipi paṭhamajjhānasamādhi, paṭhamajjhānasamādhi  aiva maggakkhaṇaipi, tathā pubbabhāgaipi catutthajjhānasamādhi, catutthajjhānasamādhi aiva maggakkhaṇaipīti atthau|

‘‘kiṃ panāyaṃ maggadhammānaṃ daisanānuk‍kamau, kaivalaṃ vācāya kamavattinibhāvatau, udāhu kañ‍ci visaisaṃ upādāyā’’ti vicāraṇāyaṃ kañ‍ci visaisaṃ upādāyāti dassaintau āha – ‘‘imaisū’’tiādi| tattha bhāvanānubhāvā hitaphalāya sātisayaṃ tikkhavisadabhāvappattiyā acchariyabbhutasamatthatāyaugaina sabbasau paṭipakkhavidhamanaina yāthāvatau dhammasabhāvabaudhanaina ca sammādiṭṭhiyā bahukāratā vaiditabbā| taināha – ‘‘ayaṃ hī’’tiādi|

tassāti  sammādiṭṭhiyā| bahukārauti dhammasampaṭivaidhai bahūpakārau| idāni tamatthaṃ upamāhi vibhāvaituṃ, ‘‘yathā hī’’tiādi vuttaṃ|

vacībhaidassa kārakau vitak‍kau sāvaj‍jānavaj‍javacībhaidanivattanappavattanākārāya sammāvācāyapi upakārakau aivāti āha – ‘‘svāyaṃ…pai॰… sammāvācāyapi upakārakau’’ti| sammāsaṅkappau hi sac‍cavācāya virativācāyapi visaisapac‍cayau micchāsaṅkappatadaikaṭṭhakilaisappahānatau|

saṃvidahitvātiādīsu sammā vidahanaṃ kammantappayaujanañ‍ca aikantānavaj‍javacīkāyakammavasaina icchitabbanti virativācāvasaina saṃvidahanaṃ viratikammantassaiva payaujanañ‍ca nidassitanti daṭṭhabbaṃ| aivaṃ hissa sammāvācāya sammākammantassāpi bahukāratā jautitā siyā| vacībhaidaniyāmikā hi vacīduc‍caritavirati kāyikakiriyaniyāmikāya kāyaduc‍caritaviratiyā upakārikā| tathā hi visaṃvādanādimicchāvācatau aviratau micchākammantataupi na viramataiva| yathāha – ‘‘aikaṃ dhammaṃ atītassa…pai॰… natthi pāpaṃ akāriya’’nti| tasmā avisaṃvādanādisammāvācāya ṭhitau sammākammantampi pūraitiyaivāti vacīduc‍caritavirati kāyaduc‍caritaviratiyā upakārikā|

yasmā ājīvapārisuddhi nāma dussīlyappahānapubbikā, tasmā sammāvācākammantānantaraṃ sammāājīvau daisitauti dassaituṃ – ‘‘catubbidhaṃ panā’’tiādi  vuttaṃ| aittāvatāti parisuddhasīlājīvikāmattaina| idaṃ vīriyanti catusammappadhānavīriyaṃ|

vīriyārambhaupi sammāsatipariggahitau aiva nibbānāvahau, na kaivalauti dassaituṃ – ‘‘tatau’’tiādi vuttaṃ| sūpaṭṭhitāti bahiddhāvikkhaipaṃ pahāya suṭṭhu upaṭṭhitā kātabbā| samādhissa upakāradhammā nāma yathāvuttavatthuvisadakiriyādayau| tappaṭipakkhatau anupakāradhammā vaiditabbā| gatiyauti nipphattiyau| samanvaisitvāti sammā pariyaisitvā|

427. yathā itthīsu kathā pavattā adhitthīti vuc‍cati, aivaṃ attānaṃ adhikic‍ca pavattā ajjhattaṃ| ‘‘aivaṃ pavattamānā mayaṃ ‘attā’ti gahaṇaṃ gamissāmā’’ti iminā viya adhippāyaina attānaṃ adhikic‍ca uddissa pavattā sattasantatipariyāpan‍nā ajjhattaṃ| tasmiṃ ajjhattarūpai, attanau kaisādivatthukai kasiṇarūpaiti atthau| parikammavasaina ajjhattaṃ rūpasañ‍ñīti parikammakaraṇavasaina  ajjhattaṃ rūpasañ‍ñī, na appanāvasaina| na hi paṭibhāganimittārammaṇā appanā ajjhattavisayā sambhavati| taṃ pana ajjhattaṃ parikammavasaina laddhaṃ kasiṇanimittaṃ avisuddhamaiva hauti, na bahiddhā parikammavasaina laddhaṃ viya visuddhaṃ| taināha – ‘‘taṃ panā’’tiādi|

yassaivaṃ parikammaṃ ajjhattaṃ uppan‍nanti yassa puggalassa aivaṃ vuttappakāraina ajjhattaṃ parikammaṃ jātaṃ| nimittaṃ pana bahiddhāti paṭibhāganimittaṃ sasantatipariyāpan‍naṃ na hautīti bahiddhā| parittānīti yathāladdhāni suppasarāvamattāni| taināha – ‘‘avaḍḍhitānī’’ti| parittavasainaivāti vaṇṇavasaina ābhaugai vij‍jamānaipi parittavasainaiva idaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ vuttaṃ parittatā haittha abhibhavanassa kāraṇaṃ| vaṇṇābhaugai satipi asatipi abhibhavatīti abhibhu, parikammaṃ, ñāṇaṃ vā| abhibhu āyatanaṃ aitassāti abhibhāyatanaṃ, jhānaṃ| abhibhavitabbaṃ vā ārammaṇasaṅkhātaṃ āyatanaṃ aitassāti abhibhāyatanaṃ| atha vā ārammaṇābhibhavanatau abhibhu ca taṃ āyatanañ‍ca yauginau sukhavisaisānaṃ adhiṭṭhānabhāvatau manāyatanadhammāyatanabhāvatau cāti sasampayuttajjhānaṃ abhibhāyatanaṃ| abhibhāyatanabhāvanā nāma tikkhapañ‍ñassaiva sambhavati, na itarassāti āha – ‘‘ñāṇuttarikau puggalau’’ti| abhibhavitvā samāpaj‍jatīti aittha abhibhavanaṃ  samāpaj‍janañ‍ca upacārajjhānādhigamanasamanantaramaiva appanājhānuppādananti āha – ‘‘saha nimittuppādainaivaittha appanaṃ pāpaitī’’ti| saha nimittuppādaināti ca appanāparivāsābhāvassa lakkhaṇavacanamaitaṃ| yau khippābhiñ‍ñauti vuc‍cati, tataupi ñāṇuttarassaiva abhibhāyatanabhāvanā| aitthāti aitasmiṃ nimittai| appanaṃ pāpaitīti bhāvanāappanaṃ naiti|

aittha ca kaici ‘‘uppan‍nai upacārajjhānai taṃ ārabbha yai haiṭṭhimantaina dvai tayau javanavārā pavattanti, tai upacārajjhānapakkhikā aiva, tadanantaraṃ bhavaṅgaparivāsaina upacārasaivanāya ca vinā appanā hauti, saha nimittuppādainaiva appanaṃ pāpaitī’’ti vadanti, taṃ taisaṃ matimattaṃ| na hi pārivāsikaparikammaina appanāvārau icchitau, nāpi mahaggatappamāṇajjhānaisu viya upacārajjhānai aikantatau pac‍cavaikkhaṇā icchitabbā| tasmā upacārajjhānādhigamatau paraṃ katipayabhavaṅgacittāvasānai appanaṃ pāpuṇantau ‘‘saha nimittuppādainaivaittha appanaṃ pāpaitī’’ti vuttau| saha nimittuppādaināti ca adhippāyikamidaṃ vacanaṃ, na nītatthaṃ, tattha adhippāyau vuttanayainaiva vaiditabbau| na antausamāpattiyaṃ tadā tathārūpassa ābhaugassa asambhavatau| samāpattitau vuṭṭhitassa ābhaugau pubbabhāgabhāvanāvasaina jhānakkhaṇai pavattaṃ abhibhavanākāraṃ gahaitvā pavattauti daṭṭhabbaṃ| abhidhammaṭṭhakathāyaṃ pana ‘‘imināssa pubbābhaugau kathitau’’ti vuttaṃ| antausamāpattiyaṃ  tadā tathā ābhaugābhāvai kasmā jhānasañ‍ñāyapīti vuttanti āha – ‘‘abhibhavasañ‍ñā hissa antausamāpattiyampi atthī’’ti|

vaḍḍhitappamāṇānīti vipulappamāṇānīti atthau, na aikaṅguladvaṅgulādivaḍḍhiṃ pāpitāni tathā vaḍḍhanassaivaittha asambhavatau| taināha – ‘‘mahantānī’’ti| bhattavaḍḍhitakanti bhuñ‍janabhājanai vaḍḍhaitvā din‍naṃ bhattaṃ, aikāsanai purisaina bhuñ‍jitabbabhattatau upaḍḍhabhattanti atthau|

rūpai sañ‍ñā rūpasañ‍ñā, sā assa atthīti rūpasañ‍ñī, na rūpasañ‍ñī arūpasañ‍ñī| sañ‍ñāsīsaina jhānaṃ vadati| rūpasañ‍ñāya anuppādanamaivaittha alābhitā| bahiddhāva uppan‍nanti bahiddhāvatthusmiṃyaiva uppan‍naṃ| aittha ca –

‘‘ajjhattaṃ rūpasañ‍ñī bahiddhā rūpāni passati parittāni suvaṇṇadubbaṇṇāni| ajjhattaṃ rūpasañ‍ñī bahiddhā rūpāni passati appamāṇāni suvaṇṇadubbaṇṇāni| ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī bahiddhā rūpāni passati parittāni suvaṇṇadubbaṇṇāni| ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī bahiddhā rūpāni passati appamāṇāni suvaṇṇadubbaṇṇānī’’ti (dī॰ ni॰ 3.338, 358; a॰ ni॰ 8.65; 10.29) –

aivamidha cattāri abhibhāyatanāni āgatāni| abhidhammai (dha॰ sa॰ 244-245) pana ‘‘ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñī bahiddhā rūpāni passati parittāni suvaṇṇadubbaṇṇāni, appamāṇāni suvaṇṇadubbaṇṇānī’’ti aivamāgatāni| tattha ca kāraṇaṃ abhidhammaṭṭhakathāyaṃ vuttamaiva| tathā hi vuttaṃ aṭṭhasāliniyaṃ (dha॰ sa॰ aṭṭha॰ 204) –

‘‘kasmā pana yathā suttantai ‘ajjhattaṃ rūpasañ‍ñī aikau bahiddhā rūpāni passati parittānī’tiādi vuttaṃ, aivaṃ avatvā idha catūsupi abhibhāyatanaisu ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñitāva vuttāti| ajjhattarūpānaṃ anabhibhavanīyatau| tattha vā hi idha vā bahiddhā rūpānaiva abhibhavitabbāni, tasmā tāni niyamatau vattabbānī’’ti|

tatrāpi idhapi vuttāni ‘‘ajjhattaṃ rūpasañ‍ñī ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñīti idaṃ pana satthu daisanāvilāsamattamaivā’’ti| ayaṃ panaittha adhippāyau – idha vaṇṇābhaugarahitāni sahitāni ca sabbāni parittāni suvaṇṇadubbaṇṇāni abhibhuyyāti| pariyāyakathā hi suttantadaisanāti| abhidhammai  pana nippariyāyadaisanattā vaṇṇābhaugarahitāni visuṃ vuttāni, tathā sahitāni| atthi hi ubhayattha abhibhavanavisaisauti| tathā idha pariyāyadaisanattā vimaukkhānampi abhibhavanapariyāyau atthīti ‘‘ajjhattaṃ rūpasañ‍ñī’’tiādinā paṭhamadutiyaabhibhāyatanaisu paṭhamavimaukkhau, tatiyacatutthaabhibhāyatanaisu dutiyavimaukkhau, vaṇṇābhibhāyatanaisu tatiyavimaukkhau ca abhibhavanapattitau saṅgahitau| abhidhammai pana nippariyāyadaisanattā vimaukkhābhibhāyatanāni asaṅkaratau daisaituṃ vimaukkhai vaj‍jaitvā abhibhāyatanāni kathitāni| sabbāni ca vimaukkhakic‍cāni jhānāni vimaukkhadaisanāyaṃ vuttāni| tadaitaṃ ‘‘ajjhattaṃ rūpasañ‍ñī’’ti āgatassa abhibhāyatanadvayassa abhidhammai abhibhāyatanaisu avacanatau ‘‘rūpī rūpāni passatī’’tiādīnañ‍ca sabbavimaukkhakic‍casādhāraṇavacanabhāvatau vavatthānaṃ katanti viñ‍ñāyati|

ajjhattarūpānaṃ  anabhibhavanīyatauti idaṃ abhidhammai katthacipi ‘‘ajjhattarūpāni passatī’’ti avatvā sabbattha yaṃ vuttaṃ – ‘‘bahiddhā rūpāni passatī’’ti, tassa kāraṇavacanaṃ| taina yaṃ añ‍ñahaitukaṃ suttantai ‘‘bahiddhā rūpāni passatī’’ti vacanaṃ, taṃ taina haitunā vuttaṃ| yaṃ pana daisanāvilāsahaitukaṃ ajjhattaṃ arūpasañ‍ñitāya aiva abhidhammai vacanaṃ, na tassa añ‍ñaṃ kāraṇaṃ maggitabbanti dassaiti| ajjhattarūpānaṃ anabhibhavanīyatā ca taisaṃ bahiddhārūpānaṃ viya avibhūtattā| daisanāvilāsau ca yathāvuttavavatthānavasaina vaiditabbau vainaiyyajjhāsayavasaina vij‍jamānapariyāyakathanabhāvatau| daisanāvilāsau hi nāma vainaiyyajjhāsayānurūpaṃ vij‍jamānassa ca pariyāyassa vibhāvanaṃ, na yassa kassaci, tasmā ‘‘idha pariyāyadaisanattā’’tiādinā vuttappakāraṃ vavatthānaṃ daisanāvilāsanibandhananti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

suvaṇṇadubbaṇṇānīti aitainaiva siddhattā na nīlādiabhibhāyatanāni vattabbānīti cai? na nīlādīsu katādhikārānaṃ nīlādibhāvassaiva abhibhavanakāraṇattā| na hi taisaṃ parisuddhāparisuddhavaṇṇānaṃ parittatā appamāṇatā vā abhibhavanakāraṇaṃ, atha khau nīlādibhāvau aivāti| aitaisu ca parittādikasiṇarūpaisu yaṃyaṃcaritassa imāni abhibhāyatanāni ijjhanti, taṃ dassaituṃ – ‘‘imaisu panā’’tiādi vuttaṃ|

sabbasaṅgāhikavasaināti nīlavaṇṇanīlanidassananīlanibhāsānaṃ sādhāraṇavasaina| vaṇṇavasaināti sabhāvavaṇṇavasaina| nidassanavasaināti passitabbatāvasaina, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇavīthiyā gahaitabbatāvasaina| aubhāsavasaināti sappabhāsatāya avabhāsanavasaina| vaṇṇadhātuyā vāti añ‍janarajatavatthādivaṇṇadhātuyā| laukiyānaiva rūpāvacarajjhānabhāvatau|

435. rūpīti aittha yaināyaṃ sasantatipariyāpan‍naina rūpaina saman‍nāgatau, taṃ yassa jhānassa haitubhāvaina visiṭṭharūpaṃ hauti| yaina visiṭṭhaina rūpīti vuc‍caiyya, tadaiva sasantatipariyāpan‍narūpanimittaṃ jhānaṃ| idha pana paramatthatau rūpibhāvasādhakanti āha – ‘‘ajjhattaṃ kaisādīsū’’tiādi| rūpajjhānaṃ rūpanti uttarapadalaupaina vuttaṃ – ‘‘rūpūpapattiyā’’tiādīsu (dha॰ sa॰ 160-161, 185-190 ādayau, 244-245 ādayau; vibha॰ 625) viya|

subhantvaiva  adhimuttau hautīti ayaṃ tatiyavimaukkhau| idha suparisuddhanīlādivaṇṇakasiṇajjhānavasaina vuttauti dassaitvā idāni paṭisambhidāpāḷiyaṃ tassa brahmavihārajjhānavasaina āgatabhāvaṃ dassaituṃ – ‘‘paṭisambhidāmaggai panā’’tiādi āraddhaṃ| idha pana uparipāḷiyaṃyaiva brahmavihārānaṃ āgatattā taṃ nayaṃ paṭikkhipitvā parisuddhanīlādivaṇṇakasiṇavasainaiva subhavimaukkhau anuñ‍ñātau|

443. parikammapathaviyāpīti akatāya vā katāya vā daḷhamaṇḍalādisaṅkhātaparikammapathaviyāpi| uggahanimittādīnaṃ pathavīkasiṇanti nāmaṃ nissitai nissayavauhāravasaina vuttanti daṭṭhabbaṃ, yathā ‘‘mañ‍cā uk‍kuṭṭhiṃ karauntī’’ti|

sīlānīti pātimaukkhasaṃvarādīni cattāri sīlāni| saudhaitvāti anāpaj‍janaina āpan‍navuṭṭhāpanaina kilaisaihi appaṭipīḷanaina ca visaudhaitvā| tividhañhi sīlassa visaudhanaṃ nāma – anāpaj‍janaṃ āpan‍navuṭṭhāpanaṃ kilaisaihi ca appaṭipīḷananti| kammaṭṭhānabhāvanaṃ paribundhaiti uparaudhaiti pavattituṃ na daitīti palibaudhau rakārassa lakāraṃ katvā, paribandhauti atthau| upacchinditvāti samāpan‍naina saṅgāhaṇaina vā uparundhitvā, apalibaudhaṃ katvāti atthau| kalyāṇamittaṃ upasaṅkamitvāti –

‘‘piyau garu bhāvanīyau, vattā ca vacanakkhamau|

gambhīrañ‍ca kathaṃ kattā, nau caṭṭhānai niyaujakau’’ti|| (a॰ ni॰ 7.37) –

aivamādiguṇasaman‍nāgataṃ aikantahitaisiṃ vuddhipakkhai ṭhitaṃ kalyāṇamittaṃ upasaṅkamitvā|

ananurūpaṃ  vihāranti aṭṭhārasan‍naṃ dausānaṃ añ‍ñataraina saman‍nāgataṃ| vuttañhaitaṃ aṭṭhakathāsu –

‘‘mahāvāsaṃ navāvāsaṃ, jarāvāsañ‍ca panthaniṃ|

sauṇḍiṃ paṇṇañ‍ca pupphañ‍ca, phalaṃ patthitamaiva ca||

‘‘nagaraṃ dārunā khaittaṃ, visabhāgaina paṭṭanaṃ|

pac‍cantasīmā sappāyaṃ, yattha mittau na labbhati||

‘‘aṭṭhārasaitāni ṭhānāni, iti viñ‍ñāya paṇḍitau|

ārakā parivaj‍jaiyya, maggaṃ sappaṭibhayaṃ yathā’’ti|| (visuddhi॰ 1.52)|

anurūpaiti  gaucaragāmatau nātidūranac‍cāsan‍natādīhi pañ‍cahi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatai| vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatā –

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, saināsanaṃ pañ‍caṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, saināsanaṃ nātidūraṃ hauti nac‍cāsan‍naṃ gamanāgamanasampan‍naṃ divā appākiṇṇaṃ rattiṃ appasaddaṃ appanigghausaṃ appaḍaṃsamakasavātātapasarīsapasamphassaṃ| tasmiṃ khau pana saināsanai viharantassa appakasiraina uppaj‍janti cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārā| tasmiṃ khau pana saināsanai thairā bhikkhū viharanti bahussutā āgatāgamā dhammadharā vinayadharā mātikādharā| tai kālaina kālaṃ upasaṅkamitvā paripucchati paripañhati ‘idaṃ, bhantai, kathaṃ imassa kau atthau’ti| tassa tai āyasmantau avivaṭañ‍caiva vivaranti, anuttānīkatañ‍ca uttāniṃ karaunti, anaikavihitaisu ca kaṅkhāṭhāniyaisu dhammaisu kaṅkhaṃ paṭivinaudainti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, saināsanaṃ pañ‍caṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ hautī’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 10.11)|

aittha ca nātidūraṃ nac‍cāsan‍naṃ gamanāgamanasampan‍nanti aikaṃ aṅgaṃ, divā appākiṇṇaṃ rattiṃ appasaddaṃ appanigghausanti aikaṃ, appaḍaṃsamakasavātātapasarīsapasamphassanti aikaṃ, tasmiṃ khau pana saināsanai viharantassa…pai॰… parikkhārāti aikaṃ, tasmiṃ khau pana saināsanai thairā…pai॰… kaṅkhaṃ paṭivinaudaintīti aikanti aivaṃ pañ‍caṅgāni vaiditabbāni|

khuddakapalibaudhaṃ  upacchinditvāti dīghakaisanakhalaumānaṃ chaidanaina cīvarakammacīvararajanapattapacanamañ‍capīṭhādisaudhanavasaina khuddakapalibaudhaṃ upacchinditvā|

453. uddhumātakādīsūti aittha ādi-saddaina vinīlakavipubbakavicchiddakavikkhāyitakahatavikkhittakalauhitakapuḷavakaaṭṭhikānaṃ saṅgahau daṭṭhabbau| tattha bhastā viya vāyunā uddhaṃ jīvitapariyādānā yathānuk‍kamaṃ samuggataina sūnabhāvaina dhumātattā uddhumātaṃ, uddhumātamaiva uddhumātakaṃ, paṭikūlattā vā kucchitaṃ uddhumātanti uddhumātakaṃ, tathārūpassa chavasarīrassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| vinīlaṃ vuc‍cati viparibhin‍nanīlavaṇṇaṃ, vinīlamaiva vinīlakaṃ, paṭikūlattā vā kucchitaṃ vinīlanti vinīlakaṃ, maṃsussadaṭṭhānaisu rattavaṇṇassa, pubbasan‍nicayaṭṭhānaisu saitavaṇṇassa, yaibhuyyaina ca nīlavaṇṇassa nilaṭṭhānai nīlasāṭakapārutassaiva chavasarīrassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| paribhin‍naṭṭhānaisu vissandamānapubbaṃ vipubbaṃ, vipubbamaiva vipubbakaṃ, paṭikūlattā vā kucchitaṃ vipubbanti vipubbakaṃ, tathārūpassa chavasarīrassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| vicchiddaṃ vuc‍cati dvidhā chindanaina apadhāritaṃ, vicchiddamaiva vicchiddakaṃ, paṭikūlattā vā kucchitaṃ vicchiddanti vicchiddakaṃ, vaimajjhai chin‍nassa chavasarīrassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| itau ca aittau ca vividhākāraina sauṇasiṅgālādīhi khāyitaṃ vikkhāyitaṃ, vikkhāyitamaiva vikkhāyitakaṃ, paṭikūlattā vā kucchitaṃ vikkhāyitanti vikkhāyitakaṃ, tathārūpassa chavasarīrassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ|

vividhā khittaṃ vikkhittaṃ, vikkhittamaiva vikkhittakaṃ, paṭikūlattā vā kucchitaṃ vikkhittanti vikkhittakaṃ, añ‍ñaina hatthaṃ, añ‍ñaina pādaṃ, añ‍ñaina sīsanti aivaṃ tatau tatau vikkhittassa chavasarīrassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| hatañ‍ca taṃ purimanayainaiva vikkhittakañ‍cāti hatavikkhittakaṃ, kākapadākāraina aṅgapac‍caṅgaisu satthaina hanitvā vuttanayainaiva vikkhittassa chavasarīrassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| lauhitaṃ kirati vikkhipati itau citau ca paggharatīti lauhitakaṃ, paggharitalauhitamakkhitassa chavasarīrassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| puḷavā vuc‍canti kimayau, puḷavai kiratīti puḷavakaṃ, kimiparipuṇṇassa chavasarīrassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| aṭṭhiyaiva aṭṭhikaṃ, paṭikūlattā vā kucchitaṃ aṭṭhīti aṭṭhikaṃ, aṭṭhisaṅkhalikāyapi aikaṭṭhikassapi aitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| imaisu dasasu asubhaisu paṭhamajjhānamaiva uppaj‍jati, na dutiyādīni| taināha – idha ‘‘paṭhamajjhānasahagatā sañ‍ñā’’ti| tathā hi aparisaṇṭhitajalāya sīghasautāya nadiyā arittabalainaiva nāvā tiṭṭhati, vinā arittaina na sak‍kā ṭhapaituṃ| aivamaivaṃ dubbalattā ārammaṇassa vitak‍kabalainaiva cittaṃ aikaggaṃ hutvā tiṭṭhati, vinā vitak‍kaina na sak‍kā ṭhapaituṃ| tasmā paṭhamajjhānamaivaittha hauti, na dutiyādīni| ārammaṇassa dubbalatā caittha paṭikūlabhāvaina cittaṃ ṭhapaituṃ asamatthatā|

‘‘rukkhau matau, lauhaṃ mata’’ntiādīsu yaṃ khandhappabandhaṃ upādāya rukkhādisamañ‍ñā, tasmiṃ anupacchin‍naipi al‍latādivigamanaṃ nissāya matavauhārau sammutimaraṇaṃ| saṅkhārānaṃ khaṇabhaṅgasaṅkhātaṃ khaṇikamaraṇaṃ| samucchaidamaraṇanti arahatau santānassa sabbasau ucchaidabhūtaṃ maraṇaṃ| vipassanābhāvanāvasaina caitaṃ vuttaṃ| maraṇānussatibhāvanāyaṃ pana tividhampaitaṃ nādhippaitaṃ  asaṃvaigavatthutau anupaṭṭhahanatau abāhul‍latau ca| maraṇānussatiyañhi aikaina bhavaina paricchin‍nassa jīvitindriyappabandhassa vicchaidau maraṇanti adhippaitau saṃvaigavatthutau upaṭṭhahanatau bāhul‍latau ca| idāni imamaiva maraṇaṃ sandhāya vikappantaraṃ dassaintau, ‘‘haiṭṭhā vuttalakkhaṇā vā’’tiādimāha|

asitapītādibhaidaiti asitapītakhāyitasāyitappabhaidai, asitabbakhāditabbasāyitabbavibhāgaiti atthau kālabhaidavacanicchāya abhāvatau yathā ‘‘duddha’’nti| kabaḷaṃ karīyatīti kabaḷīkārau, āharīyatīti āhārau, kabaḷīkārau ca sau āhārau cāti kabaḷīkārāhārau| vatthuvasaina caitaṃ vuttaṃ| savatthukau aiva hi āhārau idha kammaṭṭhānabhāvaina adhippaitau| aujālakkhaṇau pana āhārau aujaṭṭhamakaṃ rūpaṃ āharatīti āhārauti vuc‍cati| sau idha nādhippaitau paṭikūlākāraggahaṇassa asambhavatau| nava paṭikūlānīti gamanapariyaisanaparibhaugāsayanidānaaparipak‍kaparipak‍kaphalanissandappaṭikūlavasaina nava paṭikūlāni| samakkhanappaṭikūlaṃ pana paribhaugādīsu labbhamānattā idha visuṃ na gahitaṃ, añ‍ñathā taina saddhiṃ ‘‘dasa paṭikūlānī’’ti vattabbaṃ| visuddhimaggai (visuddhi॰ 1.303-304) pana samakkhanaṃ paribhaugādīsu labbhamānampi nissandavasaina visaisatau paṭikūlanti visuṃ gahaitvā dasahākāraihi paṭikūlatā vuttā|

uppaj‍janakasañ‍ñanti paṭikūlākāraggahaṇavasaina uppaj‍janakasañ‍ñaṃ| sañ‍ñāsaddau cāyaṃ ‘‘rūpasañ‍ñā saddasañ‍ñā’’tiādīsu (saṃ॰ ni॰ 3.57) sañ‍jānanalakkhaṇai dhammai āgatau, ‘‘anic‍casañ‍ñā dukkhasañ‍ñā’’tiādīsu vipassanāya āgatau, ‘‘uddhumātakasañ‍ñāti vā saupākarūpasañ‍ñāti vā imai dhammā aikaṭṭhā, udāhu nānaṭṭhā’’tiādīsu samathai āgatau| idha pana samathassa parikammai daṭṭhabbau| āhāraihi paṭikūlākāraggahaṇaṃ, tappabhāvitaṃ vā upacārajjhānaṃ idha ‘‘āhārai paṭikūlasañ‍ñā’’ti adhippaitaṃ|

uk‍kaṇṭhitasañ‍ñanti  nibbindanākāraina uppaj‍janakasañ‍ñaṃ| anic‍casañ‍ñanti aittha anic‍caṃ khandhapañ‍cakaṃ uppādavayañ‍ñathattabhāvatau, hutvā abhāvatau vā, tasmiṃ anic‍cai khandhapañ‍cakai anic‍canti uppaj‍jamānā anic‍calakkhaṇapariggāhikā sañ‍ñā anic‍casañ‍ñā| taināha – ‘‘pañ‍can‍naṃ upādānakkhandhāna’’ntiādi| tattha udayau  nibbattilakkhaṇaṃ, vayau vipariṇāmalakkhaṇaṃ, añ‍ñathattaṃ jarā| udayabbayañ‍ñathattaggahaṇaina anic‍calakkhaṇaṃ dassaiti| uppādavayañ‍ñathattabhāvatau hi khandhapañ‍cakaṃ anic‍canti vuc‍cati| yassa ca sabhāvaina khandhapañ‍cakaṃ anic‍canti vuc‍cati, taṃ anic‍calakkhaṇaṃ| taina hi taṃ anic‍canti lakkhīyati, anic‍calakkhaṇañ‍ca udayabbayānaṃ amanasikārā santatiyā paṭicchan‍nattā na upaṭṭhāti, udayabbayaṃ pana pariggahaitvā santatiyā vikaupitāya anic‍calakkhaṇaṃ yāthāvasarasatau upaṭṭhāti| na hi sammadaiva udayabbayaṃ sal‍lakkhaintassa pubbāpariyaina pavattamānānaṃ dhammānaṃ añ‍ñauñ‍ñabhāvaṃ sal‍lakkhaṇaina santatiyā ugghāṭitāya dhammā sambandhabhāvaina upaṭṭhahanti, atha khau ayausalākā viya asambandhabhāvaināti suṭṭhutaraṃ anic‍calakkhaṇaṃ pākaṭaṃ hauti|

‘‘yadanic‍caṃ, taṃ dukkha’’nti (saṃ॰ ni॰ 3.15, 45, 46, 76, 77, 85; 2.4.1, 4) vacanatau tadaiva khandhapañ‍cakaṃ abhiṇhappaṭipīḷanatau dukkhaṃ, abhiṇhappaṭipīḷanākārau pana dukkhalakkhaṇaṃ| tainaivāha – ‘‘anic‍cai khandhapañ‍cakai…pai॰… sañ‍ñaṃ bhāvaitī’’ti| tattha paṭipīḷanaṃ nāma yathāpariggahitaṃ udayavayavasaina saṅkhārānaṃ nirantaraṃ paṭipīḷiyamānatā vibādhiyamānatā| dukkhalakkhaṇañ‍ca abhiṇhasampaṭipīḷanassa amanasikārā iriyāpathaihi paṭicchan‍nattā na upaṭṭhāti, abhiṇhasampaṭipīḷanaṃ pana manasi karitvā iriyāpathai labbhamānadukkhappaṭicchādakabhāvai ugghāṭitai dukkhalakkhaṇaṃ yāthāvasarasatau upaṭṭhāti| tathā hi iriyāpathaihi paṭicchan‍nattā dukkhalakkhaṇaṃ na upaṭṭhāti, tai ca iriyāpathā abhiṇhasampaṭipīḷanāmanasikāraina paṭicchādakā jātā| aikasmiñhi iriyāpathai uppan‍nassa dukkhassa vinaudakaṃ iriyāpathantaraṃ tassa paṭicchādakaṃ viya hauti, aivaṃ saisāpi| iriyāpathānaṃ pana taṃtaṃdukkhapatitākārabhāvai yāthāvatau ñātai taisaṃ dukkhappaṭicchādakabhāvau ugghāṭitau nāma hauti saṅkhārānaṃ nirantaraṃ dukkhābhitun‍natāya pākaṭabhāvatau| tasmā abhiṇhasampaṭipīḷanaṃ manasi karitvā iriyāpathai labbhamānadukkhappaṭicchādakabhāvai ugghāṭitai dukkhalakkhaṇaṃ yāthāvasarasatau upaṭṭhāti|

‘‘yaṃ dukkhaṃ, tadanattā’’ti vacanatau tadaiva khandhapañ‍cakaṃ avasavattanatau anattā, avasavattanākārau pana anattalakkhaṇaṃ| taināha – ‘‘paṭipīḷanaṭṭhainā’’tiādi| anattalakkhaṇañ‍ca nānādhātuvinibbhaugassa  amanasikārā ghanaina paṭicchan‍nattā na upaṭṭhāti, nānādhātuyau pana vinibbhuj‍jitvā ‘‘añ‍ñā pathavīdhātu, añ‍ñā āpaudhātū’’tiādinā, ‘‘añ‍ñau phassau, añ‍ñā vaidanā’’tiādinā ca visuṃ visuṃ katvā ghanavinibbhaugai katai samūhaghanai kic‍cārammaṇaghanai ca bhaiditai anattalakkhaṇaṃ yāthāvasarasatau upaṭṭhāti| yā haisā añ‍ñamañ‍ñūpatthambhaisu samuditaisu rūpārūpadhammaisu aikattābhinivaisavasaina aparimadditasaṅkhāraihi mamāyamānā samūhaghanatā, tathā taisaṃ taisaṃ dhammānaṃ kic‍cabhaidassa satipi paṭiniyatabhāvai aikatau gayhamānā kic‍caghanatā, tathā sārammaṇadhammānaṃ satipi ārammaṇakaraṇabhaidai aikatau gayhamānā ārammaṇaghanatā| sā catūsu dhātūsu ñāṇaina vinibbhujitvā dissamānāsu hatthaina parimaddiyamānau phaiṇapiṇḍau viya vilīnaṃ āgacchati, yathāpac‍cayaṃ pavattamānā suñ‍ñā aitai dhammamattāti avasavattanākārasaṅkhātaṃ anattalakkhaṇaṃ pākaṭataraṃ hauti|

aparaaccharāsaṅghātavaggavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

19. kāyagatāsativaggavaṇṇanā

563. caitasā  phuṭauti cittaina pharitau| cittaina pharaṇañ‍ca samuddassa dvidhā sambhavatīti āha – ‘‘duvidhaṃ pharaṇa’’ntiādi| purimaina atthaināti ‘‘sampayaugavasaina vij‍jaṃ bhajantī’’ti vuttaina atthaina| pacchimaināti ‘‘vij‍jābhāgai vij‍jākauṭṭhāsai vattantī’’ti vuttaina|

564. mahatau saṃvaigāya saṃvattatītiādīsu ayaṃ pana aparau nayau| yāthāvatau kāyasabhāvappavaidanatau mahatau saṃvaigāya saṃvattati| atthāyāti diṭṭhadhammikādiatthāya| yaugakkhaimāyāti catūhi yaugaihi khaimabhāvāya| satisampajañ‍ñāyāti sabbattha satiavippavāsāya sattaṭṭhāniyasampajañ‍ñāya ca| ñāṇadassanappaṭilābhāyāti vipassanāñāṇādhigamāya| vij‍jāvimuttiphalasacchikiriyāyāti tissau vij‍jā cittassa adhimutti nibbānaṃ cattāri sāmañ‍ñaphalānīti aitaisaṃ pac‍cakkhakaraṇāya|

584. pañ‍ñāpaṭilābhāyātiādīsu  sauḷasasu padaisu pañ‍ñāpaṭilābhāya pañ‍ñāvuddhiyā pañ‍ñāvaipul‍lāya pañ‍ñābāhul‍lāyāti imāni cattāri pañ‍ñāvasaina bhāvavacanāni, saisāni dvādasa puggalavasaina bhāvavacanāni| sappurisasaṃsaivauti sappurisānaṃ bhajanaṃ| saddhammassavananti taisaṃ sappurisānaṃ santikai sīlādippaṭipattidīpakassa saddhammavacanassa savanaṃ| yaunisau manasikārauti sutānaṃ dhammānaṃ atthūpaparikkhāvasaina upāyaina manasikārau| dhammānudhammappaṭipattīti laukuttaradhammai anugatassa sīlādippaṭipadādhammassa paṭipaj‍janaṃ|

chan‍naṃ abhiñ‍ñāñāṇānanti iddhividhadibbasautacaitaupariyapubbainivāsadibbacakkhuāsavakkhayañāṇānaṃ| taisattatīnaṃ ñāṇānanti paṭisambhidāpāḷiyaṃ (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.1-2 mātikā) ‘‘sautāvadhānai pañ‍ñā sutamayai ñāṇaṃ, sutvāna saṃvarai pañ‍ñā sīlamayai ñāṇa’’ntiādinā ñāṇakathāya niddiṭṭhānaṃ sāvakasādhāraṇāsādhāraṇānaṃ ñāṇānaṃ| imaisañhi taisattatiñāṇānaṃ sutamayañāṇādīni sattasaṭṭhiñāṇāni sāvakassa sādhāraṇāni, ‘‘indriyaparaupariyattai ñāṇaṃ, sattānaṃ āsayānusayai ñāṇaṃ, yamakapāṭihīrai ñāṇaṃ, mahākaruṇāsamāpattiyā ñāṇaṃ, sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaṃ, anāvaraṇañāṇa’’nti (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.68-73 mātikā) imāni cha asādhāraṇañāṇāni sāvakaihi|

sattasattatīnaṃ  ñāṇānanti aittha –

‘‘jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇanti ñāṇaṃ, asati jātiyā natthi jarāmaraṇanti ñāṇaṃ| atītampi addhānaṃ jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇanti ñāṇaṃ, asati jātiyā natthi jarāmaraṇanti ñāṇaṃ| anāgatampi addhānaṃ jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇanti ñāṇaṃ, asati jātiyā natthi jarāmaraṇanti ñāṇaṃ| yampissa taṃ dhammaṭṭhitiñāṇaṃ, tampi khayadhammaṃ vayadhammaṃ virāgadhammaṃ niraudhadhammanti ñāṇaṃ| bhavapac‍cayā jātīti ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… upādānapac‍cayā bhavauti ñāṇaṃ, taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānanti ñāṇaṃ, vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhāti ñāṇaṃ, phassapac‍cayā vaidanāti ñāṇaṃ, saḷāyatanapac‍cayā phassauti ñāṇaṃ, nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatananti ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpanti ñāṇaṃ, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇanti ñāṇaṃ, avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārāti ñāṇaṃ, asati  avij‍jāya natthi saṅkhārāti ñāṇaṃ, atītampi addhānaṃ avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārāti ñāṇaṃ, asati avij‍jāya natthi saṅkhārāti ñāṇaṃ, anāgatampi addhānaṃ avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārāti ñāṇaṃ, asati avij‍jāya natthi saṅkhārāti ñāṇaṃ, yampissa taṃ dhammaṭṭhitiñāṇaṃ, tampi khayadhamaṃ vayadhammaṃ virāgadhammaṃ niraudhadhammanti ñāṇa’’nti –

bhagavatā nidānavaggai (saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.34-35) jarāmaraṇādīsu aikādasasu paṭic‍casamuppādaṅgaisu pac‍caikaṃ satta satta katvā vuttāni sattasattatiñāṇāni|

tattha dhammaṭṭhitiñāṇanti pac‍cayākārañāṇaṃ| pac‍cayākārau hi dhammānaṃ pavattisaṅkhātāya ṭhitiyā kāraṇattā ‘‘dhammaṭṭhitī’’ti vuc‍cati, tattha ñāṇaṃ dhammaṭṭhitiñāṇaṃ, ‘‘jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇa’’ntiādinā vuttassaiva chabbidhassa ñāṇassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| khayadhammanti khayagamanasabhāvaṃ| vayadhammanti vayagamanasabhāvaṃ| virāgadhammanti viraj‍janasabhāvaṃ| niraudhadhammanti nirujjhanasabhāvanti atthau|

lābhautiādīsu lābhauyaiva upasaggaina visaisaitvā ‘‘paṭilābhau’’ti vuttau| puna tassaiva atthavivaraṇavasaina ‘‘patti sampattī’’ti vuttaṃ| phusanāti adhigamanavasaina phusanā| sacchikiriyāti paṭilābhasacchikiriyā| upasampadāti nipphādanā|

sattan‍nañ‍ca  saikkhānanti tissau sikkhā sikkhantīti saikkhasañ‍ñitānaṃ sautāpattimaggaṭṭhādīnaṃ sattan‍naṃ| puthuj‍janakalyāṇakassa cāti nibbānagāminiyā paṭipadāya yuttattā sundaraṭṭhaina kalyāṇasañ‍ñitassa puthuj‍janassa| vaḍḍhitaṃ vaḍḍhanaṃ aikāyāti vaḍḍhitavaḍḍhanā| yathāvuttānaṃ aṭṭhan‍nampi pañ‍ñānaṃ vasaina visaisatauva arahatau pañ‍ñāvasaina pañ‍ñāvuddhiyā| tathā pañ‍ñāvaipul‍lāya|

yassa kassacipi visaisatau anurūpadhammassa mahantaṃ nāma kic‍casiddhiyā vaiditabbanti tadassa kic‍casiddhiyā dassaintau ‘‘mahantai atthai pariggaṇhātī’’tiādimāha| tattha atthādīnaṃ mahantabhāvau mahāvisayatāya vaiditabbau, mahāvisayatā ca taisaṃ paṭisambhidāmaggai āgatanayaina vaiditabbā| sīlakkhandhassa pana haitumahantatāya, pac‍cayamahantatāya, nissayamahantatāya, pabhaidamahantatāya, kic‍camahantatāya, phalamahantatāya, ānisaṃsamahantatāya ca mahantabhāvau vaiditabbau| tattha haitu alaubhādayau| pac‍cayau hirauttappasaddhāsativīriyādayau| nissayau sāvakabaudhipac‍caikabaudhiniyatatā taṃsamaṅginau ca purisavisaisā| pabhaidau cārittavārittādivibhāgau| kic‍caṃ tadaṅgādivasaina paṭipakkhavidhamanaṃ| phalaṃ saggasampadā nibbānasampadā ca| ānisaṃsau piyamanāpatādi| ayamaittha saṅkhaipau, vitthārau pana visuddhimaggai (visuddhi॰ 1.9) ākaṅkhaiyyasuttādīsu (ma॰ ni॰ 1.64 ādayau) ca āgatanayaina vaiditabbau| iminā nayaina samādhikkhandhādīnampi mahantatā yathārahaṃ niddhāraitvā vattabbā| ṭhānāṭṭhānādīnaṃ mahantabhāvau pana mahāvisayatāya vaiditabbau| tattha ṭhānāṭṭhānānaṃ mahāvisayatā bahudhātukasuttādīsu āgatanayaina vaiditabbā|

vihārasamāpattīnaṃ mahāvisayatā samādhikkhandhai mahāvisayatāniddhāraṇanayaina vaiditabbā, ariyasac‍cānaṃ sakalayānasaṅgāhakatau sac‍cavibhaṅgai (vibha॰ 189 ādayau) taṃsaṃvaṇṇanāsu (vibha॰ aṭṭha॰ 189 ādayau) ca āgatanayaina, satipaṭṭhānādīnaṃ satipaṭṭhānavibhaṅgādīsu (vibha॰ 355 ādayau) taṃsaṃvaṇṇanādīsu (vibha॰ aṭṭha॰ 355 ādayau) ca āgatanayaina, sāmañ‍ñaphalānaṃ mahatau hitassa mahatau sukhassa mahatau atthassa mahatau yaugakkhaimassa nipphattibhāvatau santapaṇītaatak‍kāvacarapaṇḍitavaidanīyabhāvatau, abhiñ‍ñānaṃ mahāsambhāratau mahāvisayatau mahākic‍catau mahānubhāvatau mahānipphattitau, nibbānassa madanimmadanādimahatthasiddhitau ca mahantabhāvau vaiditabbau| pariggaṇhātīti sabhāvāditau paricchij‍ja gaṇhāti jānāti, paṭivijjhatīti atthau|

puthupañ‍ñāti  aitthāpi vuttanayānusāraina atthau vaiditabbau| ayaṃ pana visaisau – puthu nānākkhandhaisu ñāṇaṃ pavattatīti ‘‘ayaṃ rūpakkhandhau nāma…pai॰… ayaṃ viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau nāmā’’ti aivaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ nānākaraṇaṃ paṭic‍ca ñāṇaṃ pavattati| taisupi aikavidhaina rūpakkhandhau…pai॰… aikādasavidhaina rūpakkhandhau| aikavidhaina vaidanākkhandhau…pai॰… bahuvidhaina vaidanākkhandhau| aikavidhaina sañ‍ñākkhandhau…pai॰… bahuvidhaina sañ‍ñākkhandhau| aikavidhaina saṅkhārakkhandhau…pai॰… bahuvidhaina saṅkhārakkhandhau| aikavidhaina viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau…pai॰… bahuvidhaina  viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhauti aivaṃ aikaikassa khandhassa aikavidhādivasaina atītādivasainapi nānākaraṇaṃ paṭic‍ca ñāṇaṃ pavattati|

puthu nānādhātūsūti ‘‘ayaṃ cakkhudhātu nāma…pai॰… ayaṃ manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu nāma| tattha sauḷasa dhātuyau kāmāvacarā, dvai cātubhūmikā’’ti aivaṃ dhātūsu nānākaraṇaṃ paṭic‍ca ñāṇaṃ pavattati| tayidaṃ upādin‍nadhātuvasaina vuttaṃ| pac‍caikabuddhānañhi dvin‍nañ‍ca aggasāvakānaṃ upādin‍nadhātūsu aiva nānākaraṇaṃ paṭic‍ca ñāṇaṃ pavattati, tañ‍ca khau aikadaisatauva, na nippadaisatau| bahiddhā anupādin‍nadhātūnaṃ nānākaraṇaṃ taisaṃ avisayauva, sabbañ‍ñubuddhānaṃyaiva pana ‘‘imāya dhātuyā ussan‍nattā imassa rukkhassa khandhau saitau hauti, imassa kāḷau, imassa maṭṭhau, imassa kharau, imassa bahalatacau, imassa sukkhatacau| imassa pattaṃ vaṇṇasaṇṭhānādivasaina aivarūpaṃ| imassa pupphaṃ nīlaṃ, pītaṃ, lauhitaṃ, audātaṃ, sugandhaṃ, duggandhaṃ, missakagandhaṃ| phalaṃ khuddakaṃ, mahantaṃ, dīghaṃ, vaṭṭaṃ, suvaṇṇaṃ, dubbaṇṇaṃ, maṭṭhaṃ, pharusaṃ, sugandhaṃ, duggandhaṃ, madhuraṃ, tittakaṃ, ambilaṃ, kaṭukaṃ, kasāvaṃ| kaṇṭakau tikhiṇau, atikhiṇau, ujukau, kuṭilau, tambau, nīlau, lauhitau, audātau’’tiādinā dhātunānattaṃ paṭic‍ca ñāṇaṃ pavattati|

puthu nānāāyatanaisūti ‘‘idaṃ cakkhāyatanaṃ nāma…pai॰… idaṃ dhammāyatanaṃ nāma| tattha dasāyatanā kāmāvacarā, dvai cātubhūmakā’’ti aivaṃ āyatananānattaṃ paṭic‍ca ñāṇaṃ pavattati|

puthu nānāpaṭic‍casamuppādaisūti ajjhattabahiddhābhaidatau santānabhaidatau ca nānappabhaidaisu paṭic‍casamuppādaṅgaisu| avij‍jādiaṅgāni hi pac‍caikaṃ paṭic‍casamuppādasañ‍ñitāni| taināha – saṅkhārapiṭakai ‘‘dvādasa pac‍cayā dvādasa paṭic‍casamuppādā’’ti (saṃ॰ ni॰ ṭī॰ 1.1.110)|

puthu nānāsuñ‍ñatamanupalabbhaisūti nānāsabhāvaisu nic‍casārādivirahitaisu suñ‍ñasabhāvaisu, tatau aiva itthipurisaattattaniyādivasaina anupalabbhamānasabhāvaisu| ma-kārau haittha padasandhikarau|

puthu  nānāatthaisūti atthapaṭisambhidāvisayaisu pac‍cayuppan‍nādinānāatthaisu| dhammaisūti dhammapaṭisambhidāvisayaisu pac‍cayādinānādhammaisu| niruttīsūti  taisaṃyaiva atthadhammānaṃ niddhāraṇavacanasaṅkhātaisu nānāniruttīsu| puthu nānāpaṭibhānaisūti atthapaṭisambhidādivisayaisu imāni ñāṇāni idamatthajautakānīti tathā tathā paṭibhānatau upatiṭṭhanatau paṭibhānānīti laddhanāmaisu nānāñāṇaisu|

puthu nānāsīlakkhandhaisūtiādīsu sīlassa puthuttaṃ nānattañ‍ca vuttamaiva, itaraisaṃ pana vuttanayānusāraina suviñ‍ñaiyyattā pākaṭamaiva| yaṃ pana abhin‍naṃ aikamaiva nibbānaṃ, tattha upacāravasaina puthuttaṃ gahaitabbanti āha – ‘‘puthu nānājanasādhāraṇai dhammai samatik‍kammā’’ti| tainassa madanimmadanādipariyāyaina puthuttaṃ paridīpitaṃ hauti|

vipulai atthaiti mahantai atthai| mahantapariyāyau hi vipulasaddau| gambhīraisūti sasādīhi viya mahāsamuddau anupacitañāṇasambhāraihi alabbhanaiyyappatiṭṭhaisu khandhaisu ñāṇaṃ pavattatīti visayassa gambhīratāya ñāṇassa gambhīratā vibhāvitā|

tikkhavisadabhāvādiguṇaihi asādhāraṇattā paraisaṃ pañ‍ñāya na sāmantā, atha khau suvidūravidūraiti asamantapañ‍ñā ākārassa rassattaṃ katvā| kaici ‘‘asamatthapañ‍ñā’’ti paṭhanti, taisaṃ yathāvuttaguṇaihi añ‍ñaihi asādhāraṇattā natthi aitissā kāyaci samatthanti asamatthā pañ‍ñāti yaujanā| atthavavatthānatauti atthappabhaidassa yāthāvatau san‍niṭṭhānatau| na añ‍ñau kauci sak‍kauti abhisambhavitunti ñāṇagatiyā sampāpuṇituṃ na añ‍ñau kaucipi sak‍kauti, tasmā ayaṃ suvidūravidūraiti asamantapañ‍ñā| idāni puggalantaravasaina asamantapañ‍ñaṃ vibhāvaituṃ, ‘‘puthuj‍janakalyāṇakassā’’tiādi āraddhaṃ|

‘‘pañ‍ñāpabhaidakusalau abhin‍nañāṇau adhigatappaṭisambhidau catuvaisāraj‍jappattau dasabaladhārī purisāsabhau purisasīhau purisanāgau purisājañ‍ñau purisadhaurayhau anantañāṇau anantataijau anantayasau aḍḍhau mahaddhanau balavā naitā vinaitā anunaitā pañ‍ñāpaitā vinijjhāpaitā paikkhatā pasādaitā| sau hi bhagavā anuppan‍nassa maggassa uppādaitā, asañ‍jātassa maggassa sañ‍janaitā, anakkhātassa maggassa akkhātā, maggañ‍ñū maggavidū maggakauvidau| maggānugāmī ca pana aitarahi sāvakā viharanti pacchā saman‍nāgatā|

‘‘sau  hi bhagavā jānaṃ jānāti, passaṃ passati, cakkhubhūtau ñāṇabhūtau dhammabhūtau brahmabhūtau vattā pavattā atthassa nin‍naitā amatassa dātā dhammassāmī tathāgatau, natthi tassa bhagavatau añ‍ñātaṃ adiṭṭhaṃ aviditaṃ asacchikataṃ aphassitaṃ pañ‍ñāya| atītaṃ anāgataṃ pac‍cuppan‍naṃ upādāya sabbai dhammā sabbākāraina buddhassa bhagavatau ñāṇamukhai āpāthaṃ āgacchanti, yaṃ kiñ‍ci naiyyaṃ nāma atthi taṃ sabbaṃ jānitabbaṃ, attatthau vā paratthau vā ubhayatthau vā diṭṭhadhammikau vā atthau samparāyikau vā atthau uttānau vā atthau gambhīrau vā atthau gūḷhau vā atthau paṭicchan‍nau vā atthau naiyyau vā atthau nītau vā atthau anavaj‍jau vā atthau nik‍kilaisau vā atthau vaudānau vā atthau paramatthau vā atthau, sabbaṃ taṃ antaubuddhañāṇai parivattati|

‘‘sabbaṃ kāyakammaṃ buddhassa bhagavatau ñāṇānuparivatti, sabbaṃ vacīkammaṃ buddhassa bhagavatau ñāṇānuparivatti, sabbaṃ manaukammaṃ buddhassa bhagavatau ñāṇānuparivatti| atītai buddhassa bhagavatau appaṭihataṃ ñāṇaṃ, anāgatai buddhassa bhagavatau appaṭihataṃ ñāṇaṃ, pac‍cuppan‍nai buddhassa bhagavatau appaṭihataṃ ñāṇaṃ, yāvatakaṃ naiyyaṃ, tāvatakaṃ ñāṇaṃ| yāvatakaṃ ñāṇaṃ, tāvatakaṃ naiyyaṃ| naiyyapariyantikaṃ ñāṇaṃ, ñāṇapariyantikaṃ naiyyaṃ, naiyyaṃ atik‍kamitvā ñāṇaṃ nappavattati, ñāṇaṃ atik‍kamitvā naiyyapathau natthi, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapariyantaṭṭhāyinau tai dhammā, yathā dvin‍naṃ samuggapaṭalānaṃ sammā phusitānaṃ haiṭṭhimaṃ samuggapaṭalaṃ uparimaṃ nātivattati, uparimaṃ samuggapaṭalaṃ haiṭṭhimaṃ nātivattati, añ‍ñamañ‍ñapariyantaṭṭhāyinau, aivamaivaṃ buddhassa bhagavatau naiyyañ‍ca ñāṇañ‍ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñapariyantaṭṭhāyinau| yāvatakaṃ naiyyaṃ, tāvatakaṃ ñāṇaṃ| yāvatakaṃ ñāṇaṃ, tāvatakaṃ naiyyaṃ, naiyyapariyantikaṃ ñāṇaṃ, ñāṇapariyantikaṃ naiyyaṃ, naiyyaṃ atik‍kamitvā ñāṇaṃ nappavattati, ñāṇaṃ atik‍kamitvā naiyyapathau natthi| añ‍ñamañ‍ñapariyantaṭṭhāyinau tai dhammā| sabbadhammaisu buddhassa bhagavatau ñāṇaṃ pavattati|

‘‘sabbai  dhammā buddhassa bhagavatau āvaj‍janappaṭibaddhā ākaṅkhappaṭibaddhā manasikārappaṭibaddhā cittuppādappaṭibaddhā, sabbasattaisu buddhassa bhagavatau ñāṇaṃ pavattati| sabbaisaṃ sattānaṃ buddhau āsayaṃ jānāti, anusayaṃ jānāti, caritaṃ jānāti, adhimuttiṃ jānāti, apparajakkhai mahārajakkhai tikkhindriyai mudindriyai svākārai dvākārai suviñ‍ñāpayai duviñ‍ñāpayai bhabbā sabbai sattai pajānāti, sadaivakau laukau  samārakau sabrahmakau sassamaṇabrāhmaṇī pajā sadaivamanussā antaubuddhañāṇai parivattati|

‘‘yathā yai kaici macchakacchapā antamasau timitimiṅgalaṃ upādāya antaumahāsamuddai parivattanti, aivamaiva sadaivakau laukau samārakau sabrahmakau sassamaṇabrāhmaṇī pajā sadaivamanussā antaubuddhañāṇai parivattati| yathā yai kaici pakkhinau antamasau garuḷaṃ vainataiyyaṃ upādāya ākāsassa padaisai parivattanti, aivamaiva yaipi tai sāriputtasamā pañ‍ñāya, taipi buddhañāṇassa padaisai parivattanti, buddhañāṇaṃ daivamanussānaṃ pañ‍ñaṃ pharitvā atighaṃsitvā tiṭṭhati| yaipi tai khattiyapaṇḍitā brāhmaṇapaṇḍitā gahapatipaṇḍitā samaṇapaṇḍitā nipuṇā kataparappavādā vālavaidhirūpā, vaubhindantā mañ‍ñai caranti pañ‍ñāgataina diṭṭhigatāni, tai tai pañhaṃ abhisaṅkharitvā abhisaṅkharitvā’’ti (paṭi॰ ma॰ 3.5) –

ādinā niddiṭṭhapāḷiṃ paiyyālamukhaina saṃkhipitvā dassaintau ‘‘pañ‍ñāpabhaidakusalau pabhin‍nañāṇau…pai॰… tai pañhaṃ abhisaṅkharitvā abhisaṅkharitvā’’tiādimāha|

tattha pabhin‍nañāṇauti atthādīsu pabhaidagatañāṇau| ‘‘pabhaidañāṇau’’tipi paṭhanti, sauyaiva atthau| tai pañhanti tai tai attanā adhippaitaṃ pañhaṃ| niddiṭṭhakāraṇāti vissaj‍jitakāraṇā| upakkhittakāti bhagavatau pañ‍ñāvaiyyattiyaina samīpai khittakā antaivāsikā sampaj‍janti|

bhavati  abhibhavatīti bhūri| kiṃ? rāgādiṃ| upasaggai satipi tadaiva padaṃ tamatthaṃ vadatīti upasaggaina vināpi sau atthau viñ‍ñaiyyau anaikatthattā dhātūnanti vuttaṃ – ‘‘abhibhuyyatī’’ti| kārakabyattayaina caitaṃ vuttaṃ, tasmā rāgaṃ abhibhuyyatīti sā sā maggapañ‍ñā attanā attanā vajjhaṃ rāgaṃ abhibhuyyati abhibhavati, madatīti atthau| abhibhavatīti sā sā phalapañ‍ñā taṃ taṃ rāgaṃ bhavi abhibhavi maddīti bhūripañ‍ñā| ‘‘abhibhavitā’’ti vā pāṭhau, ‘‘abhibhavitvā’’tipi paṭhanti| abhibhavitvāti ca kiriyāya siddhabhāvadassanaṃ| pañ‍ñā cai siddhā, rāgābhibhavau ca siddhau aivāti| saisaisupi aisaiva nayau|

rāgādīsu pana raj‍janalakkhaṇau rāgau| dussanalakkhaṇau dausau| muyhanalakkhaṇau mauhau| kujjhanalakkhaṇau  kaudhau, upanandhanalakkhaṇau upanāhau| pubbakālaṃ kaudhau, aparakālaṃ upanāhau| paraguṇamakkhanalakkhaṇau makkhau, yugaggāhalakkhaṇau palāsau| parasampattikhīyanalakkhaṇā issā, attanau sampattiniggūhanalakkhaṇaṃ macchariyaṃ| attanā katapāpappaṭicchādanalakkhaṇā māyā, attanau avij‍jamānaguṇappakāsanalakkhaṇaṃ sāṭhaiyyaṃ| cittassa uddhumātabhāvalakkhaṇau thambhau, karaṇuttariyalakkhaṇau sārambhau| un‍natilakkhaṇau mānau, abbhun‍natilakkhaṇau atimānau| mattabhāvalakkhaṇau madau, pañ‍cakāmaguṇaisu cittavaussaggalakkhaṇau pamādau| bhavati abhibhavati arinti bhūri asarūpatau parassa akārassa laupaṃ katvā| taināha – ‘‘ariṃ maddanipañ‍ñāti bhūripañ‍ñā’’ti| bhavati aittha thāvarajaṅgamanti bhūri vuc‍cati pathavī yathā ‘‘bhūmī’’ti bhūri viyāti bhūripañ‍ñā vitthatavipulaṭṭhaina sabbaṃ sahatāya ca| taināha – ‘‘tāyā’’tiādi| tattha pathavisamāyāti vitthatavipulaṭṭhainaiva pathavisamāya| vitthatāyāti pajānitabbai visayai vitthatāya, na aikadaisai vattamānāya| vipulāyāti uḷārabhūtāya| saman‍nāgatauti puggalau| iti-saddau kāraṇatthai, iminā kāraṇaina puggalassa bhūripañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatattā tassa pañ‍ñā bhūripañ‍ñā nāmāti atthau| ‘‘bhūripañ‍ñassa pañ‍ñā bhūripañ‍ñapañ‍ñā’’ti vattabbai aikassa pañ‍ñāsaddassa laupaṃ katvā ‘‘bhūripañ‍ñā’’ti vuttaṃ|

apicāti  pañ‍ñāpariyāyadassanatthaṃ vuttaṃ| pañ‍ñāyamaitanti pañ‍ñāya aitaṃ| adhivacananti adhikaṃ vacanaṃ| bhūrīti bhūtai atthai khandhādikai ramati sac‍casabhāvaina, diṭṭhi viya na abhūtaiti bhūri| maidhāti asani viya siluc‍cayai kilaisai maidhati hiṃsatīti maidhā, khippaṃ gahaṇadhāraṇaṭṭhaina vā maidhā| pariṇāyikāti yassuppaj‍jati, taṃ sattaṃ attahitappaṭipattiyaṃ sampayuttadhammai ca yāthāvalakkhaṇappaṭivaidhai pariṇaitīti pariṇāyikā| imaihaiva añ‍ñānipi pañ‍ñāpariyāyavacanāni haunti|

pañ‍ñābāhul‍lanti pañ‍ñā bahulā assāti pañ‍ñābahulau, tassa bhāvau pañ‍ñābāhul‍laṃ| tañ‍ca pañ‍ñāya bāhul‍laṃ pavatti aivāti tamatthaṃ pañ‍ñāgarukassa puggalassa vasaina dassaintau, ‘‘idhaikac‍cau pañ‍ñāgarukau hautī’’tiādimāha| tattha idhaikac‍cauti puthuj‍janakalyāṇakau, ariyau vā| pañ‍ñā garu aikassāti pañ‍ñāgarukau| pañ‍ñāya caritau pavattitau pañ‍ñācaritau, pañ‍ñāya caritaṃ pavattaṃ assāti vā pañ‍ñācaritau| anulaumikakhantiādivibhāgā pañ‍ñā āsayau aitassāti pañ‍ñāsayau| pañ‍ñāya adhimuttau tan‍nin‍nauti pañ‍ñādhimuttau| samussitaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñā dhajau aitassāti pañ‍ñādhajau| pañ‍ñākaitūti tassaiva vaivacanaṃ| pañ‍ñānimittaṃ ādhipataiyyaṃ aitassāti pañ‍ñādhipataiyyau| pañ‍ñāsaṅkhātau vicayau, dhammasabhāvavicinanaṃ vā bahulaṃ aitassāti vicayabahulau| nānappakāraina dhammasabhāvavicinanaṃ bahulaṃ assāti pavicayabahulau| aukkhāyanaṃ yāthāvatau dhammānaṃ upaṭṭhānaṃ bahulaṃ aitassāti aukkhāyanabahulau| pañ‍ñāya tassa tassa dhammassa sammāpaikkhanā sampaikkhā, sampaikkhāya ayanaṃ pavattanaṃ sampaikkhāyanaṃ, sampaikkhāyanaṃ dhammau pakati assāti sampaikkhāyanadhammau| sampaikkhāyanaṃ vā yāthāvatau dassanadhammau sabhāvau aitassāti sampaikkhāyanadhammau| sabbaṃ dhammajātaṃ vibhūtaṃ vibhāvitaṃ katvā viharaṇasīlauti vibhūtavihārī|

tac‍caritautiādīsu taṃ-saddaina pañ‍ñā pac‍cāmaṭṭhā, tasmā tattha ‘‘pañ‍ñācaritau’’tiādinā atthau vaiditabbau| sā pañ‍ñā caritā garukā bahulā assāti tac‍caritau taggarukau tabbahulau| tassaṃ pañ‍ñāyaṃ nin‍nau pauṇau pabbhārau adhimuttauti tan‍nin‍nau tappauṇau tappabbhārau tadadhimuttau| sā pañ‍ñā adhipati tadadhipati, tadadhipatitau āgatau  tadādhipataiyyau| pañ‍ñāgarukautiādīni ‘‘kāmaṃ saivantaṃyaiva jānāti, ayaṃ puggalau naikkhammagarukau’’tiādīsu (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.114) viya purimajātitau pabhuti vuttāni| tac‍caritautiādīni imissā jātiyā vuttāni| idāni vuttamaivatthaṃ nidassanavasainapi dassaituṃ – ‘‘yathā’’tiādi vuttaṃ| aivamaivantiādīni dassitabbanigamanaṃ|

sīghapañ‍ñāti attanau visayai sīghappavattikā pañ‍ñā, yā samāraddhā attanau pañ‍ñākic‍caṃ adandhāyantī avitthāyantī khippamaiva sampāpaiti| taināha – ‘‘sīghaṃ sīghaṃ sīlāni paripūraitī’’tiādi| tattha sīghaṃ sīghanti bahūnaṃ sīlādīnaṃ saṅgahatthaṃ dvikkhattuṃ vuttaṃ| sīlānīti cārittavārittavasaina pañ‍ñattāni pātimaukkhasaṃvarasīlāni, ṭhapaitvā vā indriyasaṃvaraṃ tassa visuṃ gahitattā itarāni tividhasīlāni| indriyasaṃvaranti cakkhādīnaṃ chan‍naṃ indriyānaṃ rāgappaṭighappavaisaṃ akatvā satikavāṭaina nivāraṇaṃ thakanaṃ| bhaujanai mattañ‍ñutanti pac‍cavaikkhitaparibhaugavasaina bhaujanai pamāṇañ‍ñubhāvaṃ| jāgariyānuyauganti divasassa tīsu kauṭṭhāsaisu rattiyā paṭhamamajjhimakauṭṭhāsaisu ca jāgarati na niddāyati, samaṇadhammamaiva karautīti jāgarau, jāgarassa bhāvau, kammaṃ vā jāgariyaṃ, jāgariyassa anuyaugau jāgariyānuyaugau, taṃ jāgariyānuyaugaṃ| sīlakkhandhanti saikkhaṃ vā asaikkhaṃ vā sīlakkhandhaṃ| aivamitaraipi khandhā vaiditabbā| pañ‍ñākkhandhanti maggapañ‍ñā ca saikkhāsaikkhānaṃ laukiyapañ‍ñā ca| vimuttikkhandhanti phalavimutti| vimuttiñāṇadassanakkhandhanti pac‍cavaikkhaṇañāṇaṃ| sīghapañ‍ñāniddaisasadisauyaiva lahupañ‍ñāniddaisau, tathā hāsapañ‍ñāniddaisau| javanapañ‍ñāniddaisau pana kalāpasammasananayaina pavattau| tikkhapañ‍ñāniddaisau vīriyassa ussuk‍kāpanavasaina, nibbaidhikapañ‍ñāniddaisau  sabbalaukai anabhiratasañ‍ñāvasaina pavattau| tattha turitakiriyā sīghatā| adandhatā lahutā| vaigāyitattaṃ khippatā|

hāsabahulauti pītibahulau| saisapadāni tassaiva vaivacanāni| atha vā hāsabahulauti mūlapadaṃ| vaidabahulauti tassā aiva pītiyā sampayuttasaumanassavaidanāvasaina niddaisapadaṃ| tuṭṭhibahulauti nātibalavapītiyā tuṭṭhākāravasaina| pāmauj‍jabahulauti balavapītiyā pamuditabhāvavasaina| sīlāni paripūraitīti haṭṭhappahaṭṭhau udaggūdaggau  sampiyāyamānau sīlāni sampādaiti| pītisaumanassasahagatā hi pañ‍ñā abhirativasaina ārammaṇai phul‍litā vikasitā viya pavattati, na aivaṃ upaikkhāsahagatāti|

anic‍catau khippaṃ javatīti ‘‘khandhapañ‍cakaṃ anic‍ca’’nti vaigāyitaina pavattati, paṭipakkhadūrībhāvaina pubbābhisaṅkhārassa sātisayattā indaina vissaṭṭhavajiraṃ viya lakkhaṇaṃ avirajjhantī adandhāyantī anic‍calakkhaṇaṃ vaigasā paṭivijjhati, tasmā sā javanapañ‍ñā nāmāti atthau| saisapadaisupi aisaiva nayau| aivaṃ lakkhaṇārammaṇikavipassanāvasaina javanapañ‍ñaṃ dassaitvā balavavipassanāvasaina dassaituṃ – ‘‘rūpa’’ntiādi vuttaṃ| tattha khayaṭṭhaināti yattha yattha uppaj‍jati, tattha tatthaiva bhij‍janatau khayasabhāvattā| bhayaṭṭhaināti bhayānakabhāvatau| asārakaṭṭhaināti attasāravirahatau nic‍casārādivirahatau ca| tulayitvāti tulābhūtāya vipassanāpañ‍ñāya tulaitvā| tīrayitvāti tāya aiva tīraṇabhūtāya tīraitvā| vibhāvayitvāti yāthāvatau pakāsaitvā pākaṭaṃ katvā| atha vā tulayitvāti kalāpasammasanavasaina tulayitvā| tīrayitvāti udayabbayānupassanāvasaina tīraitvā| vibhāvayitvāti bhaṅgānupassanādivasaina pākaṭaṃ katvā| vibhūtaṃ katvāti saṅkhārupaikkhānulaumavasaina phuṭaṃ katvā| rūpaniraudhaiti rūpakkhandhassa niraudhabhūtai nibbānai| khippaṃ javatīti nin‍napauṇapabbhāravasaina javati pavattati| idāni sikhāppattavipassanāvasaina javanapañ‍ñaṃ dassaituṃ, puna ‘‘rūpa’’ntiādi vuttaṃ|

ñāṇassa tikkhabhāvau nāma savisaisaṃ paṭipakkhasamucchindanaina vaiditabbauti ‘‘khippaṃ kilaisai chindatīti tikkhapañ‍ñā’’ti vatvā tai pana kilaisai vibhāgaina dassaintau, ‘‘uppan‍naṃ kāmavitak‍ka’’ntiādimāha| samathavipassanāhi vikkhambhanatadaṅgavasaina pahīnampi ariyamaggaina asamūhatattā uppattidhammataṃ anatītatāya asamūhatuppan‍nanti vuc‍cati, taṃ idha ‘‘uppan‍na’’nti adhippaitaṃ| nādhivāsaitīti santānaṃ āraupaitvā na vāsaiti| pajahatīti samucchaidavasaina pajahati| vinaudaitīti khipati| byantiṃ karautīti vigatantaṃ karauti| anabhāvaṃ gamaitīti anu abhāvaṃ  gamaiti, vipassanāk‍kamaina ariyamaggaṃ patvā samucchaidavasainaiva abhāvaṃ gamayatīti atthau| aittha ca kāmappaṭisaṃyuttau vitak‍kau kāmavitak‍kau| ‘‘imai sattā  marantū’’ti paraisaṃ maraṇappaṭisaṃyuttau vitak‍kau byāpādavitak‍kau| ‘‘imai sattā vihiṃsiyantū’’ti paraisaṃ vihiṃsāpaṭisaṃyuttau vitak‍kau vihiṃsāvitak‍kau| pāpakaiti lāmakai| akusalai dhammaiti akausal‍lasambhūtai dhammai| tikkhapañ‍ñau nāma khippābhiñ‍ñau hauti, paṭipadā cassa na calatīti āha – ‘‘aikamhi āsanai cattārau ariyamaggā’’tiādi|

‘‘sabbai saṅkhārā anic‍cā dukkhā vipariṇāmadhammā saṅkhatā paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā khayadhammā vayadhammā virāgadhammā niraudhadhammā’’ti yāthāvatau dassanaina sac‍cappaṭivaidhau ijjhati, na añ‍ñathāti kāraṇamukhaina nibbaidhikapañ‍ñaṃ dassaituṃ – ‘‘sabbasaṅkhāraisu ubbaigabahulau hautī’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tattha ubbaigabahulauti ‘‘sabbai saṅkhārā anic‍cā’’tiādinā (dha॰ pa॰ 277) nayaina sabbasaṅkhāraisu abhiṇhappavattasaṃvaigau| uttāsabahulauti ñāṇabhayavasaina sabbasaṅkhāraisu bahusau utrastamānasau| taina ādīnavānupassanamāha| uk‍kaṇṭhanabahulauti saṅkhāratau uddhaṃ visaṅkhārābhimukhatāya uk‍kaṇṭhanabahulau| iminā nibbidānupassanamāha| aratibahulautiādinā tassā aiva aparāparūpapattiṃ| bahimukhauti sabbasaṅkhāratau bahibhūtaṃ nibbānaṃ uddissa pavattañāṇamukhau| tathā pavattitavimaukkhamukhau| nibbijjhanaṃ paṭivijjhanaṃ nibbaidhau, sau aitissā atthīti nibbaidhikā, nibbijjhatīti vā nibbaidhikā, sā aiva pañ‍ñā nibbaidhikapañ‍ñā| anibbiddhapubbanti anamataggai saṃsārai antaṃ pāpaitvā anividdhapubbaṃ| appadālitapubbanti tassaiva atthavacanaṃ, antakaraṇainaiva appadālitapubbanti atthau| laubhakkhandhanti laubharāsiṃ, laubhakauṭṭhāsaṃ vā|

kāyagatāsativaggavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

20. amatavaggavaṇṇanā

600-611. natthi  aittha mataṃ maraṇaṃ vināsauti amataṃ, nibbānanti āha – ‘‘maraṇavirahitaṃ nibbānaṃ paribhuñ‍jantī’’ti| amatassa vā nibbānassa adhigamahaitutāya  amatasadisaatappakasukhapatitatāya ca kāyagatāsati ‘‘amata’’nti vuttā| paribhuñ‍jantīti jhānasamāpaj‍janaina vaḷañ‍janti| viraddhanti anadhigamaina virajjhitaṃ| taināha – ‘‘virādhitaṃ nādhigata’’nti| āraddhanti sādhitaṃ nipphāditaṃ| tañ‍ca paripuṇṇaṃ nāma hautīti āha – ‘‘āraddhanti paripuṇṇa’’nti| pamādiṃsūti kālabyattayainaidaṃ vuttanti āha – ‘‘pamaj‍jantī’’ti|

amatavaggavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

iti manaurathapūraṇiyā aṅguttaranikāya-aṭṭhakathāya

aikakanipātavaṇṇanāya anuttānatthadīpanā samattā|

paṭhamau bhāgau niṭṭhitau|
